<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Git commands I run before reading any code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在读代码前我会执行的 Git 命令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2244</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 477</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Before diving into a codebase, I run a concise set of Git commands to map the repo’s history, structure, and hotspots. The routine surfaces remotes, recent commits, file changes, and likely entry points, helping you jump straight to meaningful parts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在进入代码库前，作者先执行一小组 Git 命令来快速梳理历史、结构与关键区域。这个流程能快速显示远端、最近提交、改动文件以及潜在入口点，帮助开发者直接进入重点代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/cover_hu3f66e25b7571f7e32d40f355f31a2ca9_56928_1200x630_resize_box_2.png' data-full='https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/cover_hu3f66e25b7571f7e32d40f355f31a2ca9_56928_1200x630_resize_box_2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/magazine/2026/04/13/sam-altman-may-control-our-future-can-he-be-trusted' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sam Altman may control our future – can he be trusted?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨姆·奥特曼可能掌控我们的未来——能被信任吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47659135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2090</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47659135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 880</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sam Altman&#x27;s influence over AI policy and development raises questions about the balance of power and accountability in tech leadership. The piece challenges readers to weigh optimism about rapid innovation against safeguards for society, privacy, and equity. Understanding who shapes AI&#x27;s trajectory matters for engineers and policymakers alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>萨姆·奥特曼在AI领域的影响力引发关于科技领袖权力与责任平衡的讨论。文章促使读者权衡对快速创新的乐观态度与对社会、隐私和公平的保障之间的取舍。理解谁在塑造AI的未来，对工程师与政策制定者都至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/69cd326ac6ea0f4558d6e181/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/r47927.jpg' data-full='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/69cd326ac6ea0f4558d6e181/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/r47927.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bryankeller.github.io/2026/04/08/porting-mac-os-x-nintendo-wii.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I ported Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把 Mac OS X 移植到了任天堂 Wii</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47691730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1877</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47691730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal experiment porting Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii, outlining the core challenges of cross-hardware porting and what it reveals about software portability. The write-up highlights bootstrapping, driver gaps, and the kinds of trade-offs required when attempting to run a modern OS on a legacy console.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者进行了将 Mac OS X 移植到任天堂 Wii 的个人尝试，概述了跨硬件移植的核心挑战以及软件可移植性在极端约束下的意义。文章聚焦引导启动、驱动缺口，以及在旧世代主机上运行现代操作系统所需的取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/glasswing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Glasswing: Securing critical software for the AI era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>玻璃翼计划：为AI时代的关键软件提供安全防护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47679121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1502</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47679121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 810</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s Project Glasswing proposes hardened security controls for critical software in AI-enabled environments, addressing supply-chain integrity, code provenance, and runtime protections. The initiative signals a shift toward AI-era security by treating software as an active, risk-prone surface rather than a static asset.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的 Glasswing 项目提出在 AI 时代加强关键软件的安全控制，覆盖供应链完整性、代码来源可追溯性和运行时防护。该举措标志着安全从静态资产转向对AI驱动环境中高风险表面的主动防护。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/eff-leaving-x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EFF is leaving X</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EFF 将离开 X</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1409</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EFF announces departure from X, citing concerns over platform policies, data practices, and civil liberties. This move signals growing tensions between advocacy groups and major social platforms, and may influence developers and researchers who rely on the platform for distribution or outreach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电子前哨基金会宣布退出 X，表示对平台政策、数据使用等问题存在关切。这一举动凸显了公民社会组织与大型社交平台之间的日益紧张关系，并可能影响依赖该平台进行传播与研究的开发者与研究者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/Twitter-censored.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/Twitter-censored.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://obdev.at/products/littlesnitch-linux/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LittleSnitch for Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 版 Little Snitch</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47697870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47697870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 439</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Linux port of the macOS outbound firewall LittleSnitch arrives, enabling per-app network monitoring and rule-based controls for Linux users. This signals growing demand for fine-grained, user-friendly network policies on desktop Linux and could influence future firewall tooling and UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 版 Little Snitch 为桌面 Linux 增添按应用层级进行出站网络控制的能力，用户可对应用的网络请求逐个批准或阻止。这类细分的网络策略工具日益成为需求，可能推动未来防火墙工具的用户体验与实现方向。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/42796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Issue: Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks with Feb updates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 在二月更新后难以处理复杂工程任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 727</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user reports Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks after February updates, citing issues with multi-file projects, debugging, and reliability. The note highlights current limits of AI coding assistants for serious production work and the need for ongoing improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有用户反映在二月更新后，Claude Code 难以处理复杂工程任务，涉及多文件项目、调试与可靠性等问题。该评测强调了 AI 编码助手在正式生产场景中的局限性，以及持续改进的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbsnews.com/live-updates/artemis-ii-splashdown-return/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II safely splashes down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号安全落水回归</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47725583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47725583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA&#x27;s Artemis II mission completed a safe splashdown, marking a milestone in crewed lunar exploration. The return validates deep-space readiness and endurance in human spaceflight, setting the stage for subsequent lunar missions and partnerships with industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯II号完成安全落水，标志着载人月球探测的重要里程碑。此次归来验证了深空任务的可行性与人类航天的耐久性，并为后续任务及产业合作奠定基础。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aisle.com/blog/ai-cybersecurity-after-mythos-the-jagged-frontier' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Small models also found the vulnerabilities that Mythos found</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小模型也发现了 Mythos 发现的漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47732020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47732020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 321</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings show small models exhibit the same vulnerabilities Mythos uncovered in larger models, underscoring that model-size alone doesn&#x27;t grant security. This broadens risk assessment to include a wider range of models and prompts tighter evaluation and defense strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，即使是小型模型也存在 Mythos 所揭示的漏洞，说明漏洞并非只有大模型才有。这使安全评估需覆盖更广的模型范围，并推动更严格的测试与防护措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aisle.com/api/media/file/freepik_create-this_2743478282-1200x630.webp' data-full='https://aisle.com/api/media/file/freepik_create-this_2743478282-1200x630.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47719740' data-tags='Gaming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rowan441.github.io/1dchess/chess.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1D Chess</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一维棋</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 968</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>1D Chess challenges players with a single-line board, forcing rethinking of piece mobility and strategy. It’s a compact exploration of how constraints alter classical chess dynamics and makes for a playful, AI-friendly coding or game-design exercise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一维棋盘上的棋局迫使玩家重新思考移动规则与策略，极简化的设定揭示了约束如何改变经典棋局的动力学。也是一个有趣的 AI 研究/游戏设计练习，便于实现与教学。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rowan441.github.io/1dchess/assets/pieces/white-king.svg' data-full='https://rowan441.github.io/1dchess/assets/pieces/white-king.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47676509' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nasa.gov/gallery/lunar-flyby/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lunar Flyby</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>月球飞掠</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA recently showcased imagery and data from a lunar flyby, illustrating how spacecraft skim past the Moon to study gravity assists, lighting, and surface features. Such flybys refine trajectory design for future missions and provide rich science visuals for public outreach. They also help calibrate navigation systems used across deep-space operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NASA 展示来自月球近距飞掠的影像与数据，展示航天器在靠近月球时进行引力辅助、照明与表面特征研究的能力。此类飞掠有助于优化未来任务的轨道设计并为公众科普提供直观的科学画面。它们还用于校准深空任务的导航系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/art002e009288orig.jpg' data-full='https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/art002e009288orig.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jaso1024.com/mvidia/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A game where you build a GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：一个组装显卡的游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47640728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 925</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47640728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive game where players design and assemble a GPU, exploring how architectural choices affect performance. It demonstrates how approachable hardware engineering concepts can be gamified to educate and inspire tinkering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>玩家在游戏中设计并组装显卡，探索架构选择如何影响性能。该项目展示了将硬件工程概念以游戏化方式呈现的潜力，能激发学习和动手兴趣。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47661439' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.0xsid.com/blog/wont-download-your-app' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I won&#x27;t download your app. The web version is a-ok</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不想下载你的应用，网页版本就够用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47661439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 918</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47661439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 551</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A web-first stance: the author chooses not to install a native app and relies on the web version instead. Web apps can be quicker to update and lighter on device storage, reducing platform lock-in, though they may trade off certain features like push notifications and offline access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者坚持网页优先，不安装原生应用，而选择网页版本。网页应用更新更快、占用存储更少，降低了对特定平台的绑定，但在推送通知和离线功能等方面存在权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47647788' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ergosphere.blog/posts/the-machines-are-fine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The threat is comfortable drift toward not understanding what you&#x27;re doing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>威胁来自对所做之事逐渐变得难以理解的舒适漂移</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 900</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 587</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comfortable drift toward opaque automation can leave operators unaware of what their systems are actually doing. The piece argues that as tooling grows more capable, critical thinking and verification risk being outsourced to machines. Maintaining explicit mental models and observability is essential to reliability and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对自动化日益隐蔽性的舒适感可能让人对系统实际在做什么失去清晰认知。文章强调，工具越强大，批判性思维与验证越易外包给机器，需保持可观测性与清晰的认知模型以维护可靠性与安全。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47648828' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lalitm.com/post/building-syntaqlite-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Eight years of wanting, three months of building with AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 耗时三个月打造 Syntaqlite AI，终成梦想</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47648828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 888</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47648828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 279</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After eight years of ambition, a developer builds Syntaqlite AI in three months using AI tools. The post showcases how AI can accelerate prototyping and tooling, turning a long-held dream into a tangible product quickly. It also highlights pragmatic tradeoffs and how to structure AI-assisted workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在八年的野心推动下，开发者用 AI 工具仅用三个月就打造出 Syntaqlite AI。此案例展示了 AI 如何显著加速原型开发和工具链搭建，让长期的梦想在短时间内变为现实。文中也讨论了实际取舍与如何组织 AI 辅助的工作流程。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/arman-bd/guppylm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I built a tiny LLM to demystify how language models work</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47655408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 880</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47655408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tiny LLM demonstrates internal workings in an accessible way, touching on tokenization, attention, and prompt behavior. The project serves as a practical educational tool for developers curious about how large language models operate under the hood.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个小型语言模型以直观方式演示了分词、注意力等内部机制，帮助开发者直观理解大语言模型的工作原理。该项目强调从底层机制出发的教育价值。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apps.apple.com/nl/app/google-ai-edge-gallery/id6749645337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemma 4 on iPhone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemma 4 在 iPhone 上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 851</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 is now available on iPhone, expanding access to Google&#x27;s Gemini model family on mobile devices. This move highlights the shift toward on-device AI, reducing cloud latency and improving privacy for everyday users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 已可在 iPhone 上使用，标志着 Gemini 家族在移动端的进一步扩展。此举凸显了将高性能 AI 推向设备端的趋势，提升隐私保护并降低延迟。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47736555' data-tags='Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stevehanov.ca/blog/how-i-run-multiple-10k-mrr-companies-on-a-20month-tech-stack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I run multiple $10K MRR companies on a $20/month tech stack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用20美元/月的技术栈运营多家月订阅收入达一万美元的公司</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 848</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 468</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Running several SaaS-like businesses on a $20/month stack is feasible with strong automation and lean operations. The post outlines strategies to maintain roughly $10K MRR per product at tiny ongoing costs, highlighting how a low-cost, automated stack can scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在极低成本和高度自动化的条件下，仍然能经营多家以订阅为核心的产品并实现可观的月经常性收入。作者分享了将每个项目的月耗控在约20美元、个别产品实现约1万美元MRR的做法，挑战了对高成本技术栈的普遍认知。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47716043' data-tags='Open Source,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.numerique.gouv.fr/sinformer/espace-presse/souverainete-numerique-reduction-dependances-extra-europeennes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France Launches Government Linux Desktop Plan as Windows Exit Begins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国启动政府级 Linux 桌面计划，Windows 退出进程开启</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 841</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France accelerates digital sovereignty by deploying Linux desktops across government as Windows migration begins. The move aims to cut vendor lock-in, reduce costs, and improve security, while posing challenges for app compatibility and procurement. It signals a broader trend toward open-source adoption in the public sector.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国通过在政府领域全面部署 Linux 桌面来提升数字主权，Windows 退出计划随之开启。此举旨在降低厂商锁定、压缩成本并提升安全性，但在应用兼容性与采购流程上也带来挑战。反映出公共部门日益重视开源的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s3.eu-west-par.io.cloud.ovh.net/numerique-gouv-website/numerique-gouv-website/images/NUMERIQUE-GOUV_LOGO_PRIMAIRE_SIGNATURE_RVB.original.svg?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&amp;X-Amz-Credential=83d53d2d1ca64211b6b9581adffa67ce%2F20260410%2Feu-west-par%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&amp;X-Amz-Date=20260410T132142Z&amp;X-Amz-Expires=3600&amp;X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&amp;X-Amz-Signature=6f7e6dcdaa6aeb57fe15f60bb8e5564ce95029d31e0d93650bec45e36f7c10b8' data-full='https://s3.eu-west-par.io.cloud.ovh.net/numerique-gouv-website/numerique-gouv-website/images/NUMERIQUE-GOUV_LOGO_PRIMAIRE_SIGNATURE_RVB.original.svg?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&amp;X-Amz-Credential=83d53d2d1ca64211b6b9581adffa67ce%2F20260410%2Feu-west-par%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&amp;X-Amz-Date=20260410T132142Z&amp;X-Amz-Expires=3600&amp;X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&amp;X-Amz-Signature=6f7e6dcdaa6aeb57fe15f60bb8e5564ce95029d31e0d93650bec45e36f7c10b8' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Caveman: Why use many token when few token do trick</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Caveman：为何用多 Token 不如用少 Token 就能完成任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 836</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 351</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Caveman argues that you can achieve effective results with far fewer tokens than conventional language models assume. The project demonstrates token-efficient techniques that reduce compute and cost while preserving performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Caveman 主张在许多任务中无需大量标记即可取得良好效果，展示了更高效的标记方案以降低计算成本，同时保持性能。该项目提供了实现标记效率的具体方法与实践。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Mythos 预览版以 PDF 形式提供新能力与安全特性，概述 Mythos 在推理、上下文记忆与防护边界方面的实现。该预览有助于开发者评估这一版本与前代区别及在企业工作流中的定位。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jsnover.com/blog/2026/03/13/microsoft-hasnt-had-a-coherent-gui-strategy-since-petzold/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft hasn&#x27;t had a coherent GUI strategy since Petzold</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece argues that Microsoft has lacked a coherent GUI strategy since the Petzold era, citing fragmented UI decisions across Windows and related tools. It calls for a refreshed, user-centric design direction to unify experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为自 Petzold 时代起，微软在 GUI 策略上长期缺乏统一性，Windows 及相关工具的界面决策显得零散。文章倡导以用户为中心的设计方向，重塑统一的用户体验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Switzerland has 25 Gbit internet and America doesn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自由市场的谎言：瑞士为何有25 Gbit互联网，美国却没有</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 781</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 665</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique arguing that market ideals don&#x27;t explain broadband differences; Switzerland&#x27;s 25 Gbps speeds reflect targeted policy and infrastructure investment rather than pure market forces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文质疑自由市场理论对宽带差异的解释，指出瑞士的25 Gbps 互联网源于有针对性的政策引导和基础设施投资，而美国则受监管与市场结构的制约。文章强调监管选择、投资激励与市场结构如何决定一个国家的连通性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' data-full='https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnet.com/home/security/when-flock-comes-to-town-why-cities-are-axing-the-controversial-surveillance-technology/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US cities are axing Flock Safety surveillance technology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国城市正在削减Flock Safety监控技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 753</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 426</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Multiple U.S. cities are dropping Flock Safety license-plate surveillance programs amid privacy and civil-liberties concerns. The move underscores the tension between public safety aims and individual rights in a surveillance-heavy era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多座美国城市在隐私与公民自由关注下撤回Flock Safety的车牌监控项目。这一举措凸显了在高强度监控时代，公共安全目标与个人权利之间的张力。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Docker pull failure in Spain is linked to Cloudflare blocking football-related requests, illustrating how regional filtering can disrupt developer workflows. It underscores the need for resilient mirrors and alternative routing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙的 Docker 拉取失败被追踪至 Cloudflare 对足球相关流量的拦截，反映出区域性过滤可能干扰开发工作流。需要更稳健的镜像源与路由策略以缓解。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者尝试安装所有 Firefox 扩展，记录冲突、性能与可用性。结果显示扩展生态强大却易变得难以维护，强调需要更严格的扩展筛选与性能预算。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arhan.sh/blog/native-instant-space-switching-on-macos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Native Instant Space Switching on macOS</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 311</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS now supports native instant switching between virtual desktops (Spaces), drastically reducing the perceived latency when moving between spaces. It highlights implications for workflow efficiency, window management, and potential limitations compared with third-party tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 现在原生支持在虚拟桌面（Spaces）之间即时切换，显著降低切换时的延迟，提升多任务处理体验。文中讨论了这一特性对窗口管理和工作流程的影响，以及与第三方工具相比的潜在局限。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.axios.com/2026/04/09/meta-social-media-addiction-ads' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta removes ads for social media addiction litigation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta因社媒成瘾诉讼而移除广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47703419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47703419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s move to remove ads linked to a social-media addiction lawsuit underscores how litigation is nudging platforms to rethink ad-supported models and user-well-being commitments. The case could ripple through advertising strategies and policy discussions around platform responsibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 因社媒成瘾诉讼而移除相关广告，凸显法律诉讼正在推动平台重新审视广告支持模式与对用户健康的承诺。此举可能影响广告策略与对平台责任的政策讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47704804' data-tags='Space,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/news/how-nasa-built-artemis-iis-fault-tolerant-computer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How NASA built Artemis II’s fault-tolerant computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NASA 如何打造 Artemis II 的容错计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47704804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 616</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47704804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA built a fault-tolerant onboard computer for Artemis II, emphasizing resilience in radiation-rich space. The system relies on redundancy, error detection, and hardened components to keep crewed flight safe even if primary hardware fails. The design illustrates how space-grade computing priorities differ from consumer systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯II号任务的机载容错计算机采用冗余、错误检测和抗辐射组件等技术，以在关键硬件失效时仍能维持运作。该系统体现了太空计算对可靠性的高要求，远超商用系统的容错能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47719486' data-tags='Politics,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/10/france-to-ditch-windows-for-linux-to-reduce-reliance-on-us-tech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France to ditch Windows for Linux to reduce reliance on US tech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国将放弃 Windows 改用 Linux，降低对美国科技的依赖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 687</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France plans to replace Windows with Linux on government devices to reduce reliance on US tech and tighten sovereignty. The move signals growing appetite for open-source in public sector procurement and raises questions about migration complexity and vendor ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国计划把政府设备从 Windows 切换到 Linux，以减少对美国科技的依赖。这一举措凸显公共部门对开源软件的越来越大兴趣，同时也带来迁移的挑战与生态影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/france-flag-2233577438.jpg?resize=1200,873' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/france-flag-2233577438.jpg?resize=1200,873' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47672818' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idiocracy.wtf/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are We Idiocracy Yet?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们真的走向智劣时代了吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 534</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on whether modern culture is drifting toward anti-intellectualism, highlighting tech-enabled misinformation and the ethics of online discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对现代文化是否走向反智主义的挑衅性观察，聚焦科技驱动的信息误导以及网络话语的伦理问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47677853' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://z.ai/blog/glm-5.1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.1: Towards Long-Horizon Tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.1：迈向长时域任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47677853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47677853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 255</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.1 advances long-horizon reasoning, aiming to improve task planning and sustained context handling in large language models. The update signals continued the trend toward more capable multi-step problem solving, useful for complex workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.1 在长期推理能力上取得进展，提升任务规划与长上下文处理。该更新延续了让大型语言模型进行多步推理、处理复杂任务的趋势，对需要跨长文档的应用尤为有用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47664912' data-tags='Culture,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bramcohen.com/p/the-cult-of-vibe-coding-is-insane' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The cult of vibe coding is dogfooding run amok</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>氛围编码的崇拜：自我喂养文化失控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 609</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 505</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that &#x27;vibe coding&#x27;—prioritizing aesthetics, culture, and vibes over engineering rigor—has become a dogfooding trap, where teams use internal satisfaction as a proxy for quality. It warns such culture can erode product reliability, real user outcomes, and long-run sustainability. The takeaway is to balance vibe with substance and user needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文批评了所谓的“氛围编码”现象，强调注重美学与氛围而忽视工程严谨的趋势成为自我喂养的陷阱。内部测试和满足感被当成质量指标，可能侵蚀产品可靠性、真实用户价值与长期可持续性。核心是要在氛围和实质、用户需求之间取得平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93xj!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbramcohen.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1733548744%26version%3D9' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93xj!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbramcohen.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1733548744%26version%3D9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.terrybisson.com/theyre-made-out-of-meat-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>They&#x27;re made out of meat (1991)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>它们都是肉做的（1991）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47688678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 602</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47688678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aliens discover humans are meat and struggle to understand Earth&#x27;s beings; the story uses a stark premise to question assumptions about life, intelligence, and cross-cultural communication. It remains a sharp, classic piece on perspective.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>外星人发现人类其实是肉体所构成，他们努力理解地球生命与意识的边界。通过简练又讽刺的情节，质疑关于生命、智能与跨文化沟通的假设。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.terrybisson.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/TB-logo-2.png' data-full='http://www.terrybisson.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/TB-logo-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47658146' data-tags='Finance,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mining.com/france-pulls-last-gold-held-in-us-for-15b-gain/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France pulls last gold held in US</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国从美国取回最后一批黄金，获利约150亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47658146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47658146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France moved its remaining gold reserves from the United States, realizing about $15 billion in gains. The move underscores ongoing central-bank diversification away from custody risk and signals geopolitical considerations in asset custody. It also highlights how precious metals markets react to sovereign reserve shifts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国将其在美黄金储备中的最后一部分取回，实现约150亿美元的收益。这显示央行在分散储备时继续降低对单一托管方的依赖，并反映了资产保管的地缘政治考量。除此之外，此类举动往往会影响贵金属市场的供需和价格走向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47689648' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aphyr.com/posts/411-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ML promises to be profoundly weird</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机器学习将带来极其怪异的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 598</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 591</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that ML systems will exhibit unusual, sometimes counterintuitive behavior as data and objectives shift, complicating testing and reliability. The piece reflects on implications for software engineering, governance, and trust in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出机器学习系统在数据分布和目标变化下会呈现异常甚至反直觉的行为，这会让测试与可靠性变得复杂。讨论对软件工程、治理与对 AI 信任的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/0757b0f7c4d7ac7ee21b04ca91ac406a?r=pg&amp;s=96&amp;d=identicon' data-full='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/0757b0f7c4d7ac7ee21b04ca91ac406a?r=pg&amp;s=96&amp;d=identicon' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47682276' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/apr/07/trump-iran-war-ceasefire' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US and Iran agree to provisional ceasefire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美伊就临时停火达成一致</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47682276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 594</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47682276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1980</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After talks, the US and Iran reach a provisional ceasefire, signaling a de-escalation step amid ongoing tensions. The arrangement creates space for negotiations and potential humanitarian corridors, though fragility remains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经谈判，美伊就临时停火达成协议，表明紧张局势有所缓和。协议为继续谈判和人道主义走廊争取时间，但局势仍不稳固。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7cde4a66207fd4d8c625b447df29716f6e1a7235/576_0_4582_3666/master/4582.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=b2a8729bf6c3d7f318035c7c52fbec3a' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7cde4a66207fd4d8c625b447df29716f6e1a7235/576_0_4582_3666/master/4582.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=b2a8729bf6c3d7f318035c7c52fbec3a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47716490' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5mac.com/2026/04/09/fbi-used-iphone-notification-data-to-retrieve-deleted-signal-messages/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FBI used iPhone notification data to retrieve deleted Signal messages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FBI 使用 iPhone 通知数据检索已删除的 Signal 消息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 593</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forensic use of iPhone notification data enabled recovery of deleted Signal messages, highlighting how notification metadata can bypass user deletions. This underscores a growing privacy and security risk for messaging apps, and could intensify debates over data minimization and platform protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过分析 iPhone 通知数据，联邦调查局帮助找回了已删除的 Signal 消息，暴露了通知元数据可能绕过删除保护的风险。此事凸显隐私与安全之间的博弈，或将推动关于数据最小化与平台保护的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2025/12/Reddit-comment-led-police-to-identify-Brown-University-shooter.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2025/12/Reddit-comment-led-police-to-identify-Brown-University-shooter.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47698455' data-tags='Gaming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://playstarfling.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Starfling: A one-tap endless orbital slingshot game in a single HTML file</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Starfling：一个 HTML 单文件的无尽轨道弹射游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47698455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 585</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47698455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starfling is a minimalist browser game playable in a single HTML file, using a tap to control orbital slingshots. It showcases how far a tiny, self-contained web app can push interactivity and visuals with pure HTML/JS, without a build or asset pipeline. A fun example of ultra-lightweight game development and portability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Starfling 是一个极简的浏览器游戏，直接在单个 HTML 文件中就能运行，点击一次即可进行轨道弹射。它演示了使用纯 HTML/JS 就能实现丰富交互与流畅画面的极致轻量化游戏，并且无需构建工具即可带来完整体验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47690977' data-tags='Security,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/microsoft-abruptly-terminates-veracrypt-account-halting-windows-updates/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft terminates VeraCrypt account, halting Windows updates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软突然终止 VeraCrypt 账户，导致 Windows 更新中断</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47690977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47690977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports indicate Microsoft terminated a VeraCrypt account, halting Windows updates. The incident underscores how dependence on external accounts can disrupt critical software maintenance and raises questions about vendor policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称微软突然终止了VeraCrypt账户，导致Windows更新中断。这一事件凸显对外部账户的依赖如何影响关键软件维护，并引发对厂商政策的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.404media.co/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/simon-ray-TICJQELFmg4-unsplash.jpg' data-full='https://www.404media.co/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/simon-ray-TICJQELFmg4-unsplash.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47687248' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.skoda-storyboard.com/en/skoda-world/skoda-duobell-a-bicycle-bell-that-outsmarts-even-smart-headphones/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Škoda DuoBell: A bicycle bell that penetrates noise-cancelling headphones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Škoda DuoBell：一枚能穿透降噪耳机的自行车铃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 574</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 572</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Škoda&#x27;s DuoBell uses an audible signal designed to cut through active noise cancellation, ensuring cyclists are noticed. It highlights practical hardware design for urban mobility and how audible cues interact with consumer audio tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Škoda 的 DuoBell 设计成在主动降噪耳机下仍能发出清晰声音，确保骑行者被听见。该产品凸显城市出行的实用硬件设计，以及可听信号与消费级音频技术的互动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.skoda-storyboard.com/2025/07/kim_2ee84de4.png' data-full='https://cdn.skoda-storyboard.com/2025/07/kim_2ee84de4.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47739260' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pro Max 5x quota exhausted in 1.5 hours despite moderate usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pro Max 5x 配额在 1.5 小时内耗尽，使用量中等</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 565</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 517</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity. This case underscores the importance of understanding rate limits and having fallback or multi-account strategies for critical tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。此事提醒开发者在规划工作流时要考虑配额上限、突发流量以及备选方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/70dd4383923158f88ff6fabd83ff028d4bbacc173a71a94580c818b7cf90716d/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/70dd4383923158f88ff6fabd83ff028d4bbacc173a71a94580c818b7cf90716d/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47685320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 562</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47685320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 698</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adam Back&#x27;s profile in the New York Times traces attempts to uncover Satoshi Nakamoto&#x27;s true identity, weighing rumored candidates and the evidentiary curve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约时报刊文回顾 Adam Back 的追踪，试图揭示中本聪的真实身份，评估候选人及相关证据。报道揭示了这场追寻如何交织密码学史、隐私议题，以及比特币创始人神话的形成。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://updates.thunderbird.net/en-US/thunderbird/140.0/apr26-1e/donate/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Help Keep Thunderbird Alive</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thunderbird, the open-source email client, is running a fundraising drive to stay active. The appeal underscores ongoing challenges for volunteer-driven projects in sustaining development and support, even as mainstream clients rise. Donating helps ensure continued updates, privacy features, and localization support for users who value control over their data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源邮件客户端 Thunderbird 正发起募捐以维持项目运营。社区呼吁捐助以解决长期资金与维护挑战，确保未来仍具备更新、隐私功能及本地化支持。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lists.zx2c4.com/pipermail/wireguard/2026-April/009561.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WireGuard makes new Windows release following Microsoft signing resolution</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 541</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WireGuard released a Windows build aligned with a Microsoft signing resolution, addressing long-standing driver-signing hurdles. The update improves installer reliability and kernel-mode VPN performance, underscoring how platform signing policies shape open-source tooling on Windows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在微软签名决议推动下，WireGuard 推出新的 Windows 版本，解决长期的驱动签名问题。更新提升安装可靠性与内核模式下的性能，体现了平台签名策略对开源工具在 Windows 上的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47662234' data-tags='Science,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://words.filippo.io/crqc-timeline/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A cryptography engineer&#x27;s perspective on quantum computing timelines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>量子计算时间线：一位密码学工程师的观点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 541</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cryptography engineer offers a grounded view on quantum timelines, arguing that practical quantum attacks require far more qubits and error correction than currently available. The piece stresses adopting post-quantum cryptography now and planning risk mitigation as a prudent long-term strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位密码学工程师对量子计算的时间线给出务实的看法，认为要实现实用的量子攻击仍需远多于现有的量子比特与纠错能力。文章强调应尽早采用后量子密码并制定长期的风险缓解计划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/e29aa9d6-cb9e-40ee-9340-a2d128ddaabf.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/e29aa9d6-cb9e-40ee-9340-a2d128ddaabf.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Seven countries now generate all of their electricity from renewable sources, demonstrating real-world progress toward decarbonization. The story notes that reliability and grid stability still demand investment in storage and diversification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>七个国家现已实现用可再生能源发电的100%，显示出在去碳化方面的切实进展。文章也提醒，可靠性与电网稳定性仍需通过储能和多元化来解决。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wesnoth.org' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Battle for Wesnoth: open-source, turn-based strategy game</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wesnoth：开源的回合制策略游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wesnoth is a long-running open-source turn-based strategy game with a fantasy setting and a large catalog of campaigns. The project demonstrates how community-driven development can sustain a complex strategy title, with robust modding support and cross-platform availability. Its enduring viability highlights the strength of open-source ecosystems for niche, mechanics-heavy games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wesnoth 是一款长期由社区维护的开源回合制策略游戏，拥有丰富的战役和多人对战。项目展示了开源社区如何支撑复杂策略游戏的持续开发，以及跨平台和可扩展的模组生态。它的长期活力凸显了开源生态在维护高复杂性游戏中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/videos/ce3d5gkd2geo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II crew see first glimpse of far side of Moon [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿耳忒弥斯二号任务组首次揭示月球背面画面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47649721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 518</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47649721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 422</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Artemis II crew released video glimpses of the Moon&#x27;s far side, a rare up-close look during this crewed lunar test flight. This milestone underscores deep-space operations preparation, relay communications, and the planning groundwork for future crewed lunar missions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯二号任务组公布了月球背面的画面片段，这在载人绕月任务中属于难得的近距离观察。该里程碑凸显了深空作业、中继通信以及未来载人登月任务的准备工作。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/torvalds/linux/blob/master/Documentation/process/coding-assistants.rst' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI assistance when contributing to the Linux kernel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 辅助 Linux 内核贡献</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47721953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 505</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47721953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Documentation hints at AI-assisted coding guidance for Linux kernel contributors, illustrating growing acceptance of AI copilots in critical software projects. The move raises questions about tooling, governance, and code quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文档显示 Linux 内核贡献者可以在编码中获得 AI 辅助，反映 AI 助手在关键软件项目中的逐步落地。也带来关于工具治理和代码质量的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47733217' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rdi.berkeley.edu/blog/trustworthy-benchmarks-cont/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exploiting the most prominent AI agent benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>利用最显著的 AI 代理基准测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 502</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Berkeley piece critiques how prominent AI benchmarks are exploited or gamed, questioning reliability of current benchmarks for real-world agent evaluation. It argues for more robust, trustworthy benchmarks to guide research and product decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Berkeley 的文章质疑当前最显著的 AI 代理基准的健壮性，指出评测结果可能被操纵或不充分反映现实场景。它呼吁建立更可信的基准，以正确引导研究方向和产品决策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rdi.berkeley.edu/assets/images/rdi_logo_horizontal_720.png' data-full='https://rdi.berkeley.edu/assets/images/rdi_logo_horizontal_720.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47738827' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://essays.johnloeber.com/p/4-bring-back-idiomatic-design' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bring Back Idiomatic Design (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回归地道设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 500</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 260</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms. It reinforces how idiomatic design improves readability and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。强调地道设计提升可读性、安全性和开发者体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o7Gx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1d9c4a86-cb01-4f2c-ab0e-1cf2f0d0a2c8_419x304.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o7Gx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1d9c4a86-cb01-4f2c-ab0e-1cf2f0d0a2c8_419x304.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47736476' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic downgraded cache TTL on March 6th</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 在3月6日下调 Claude 的缓存 TTL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 498</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 385</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients. The change increases cache churn, potentially improving freshness at the cost of latency and API throughput. It signals ongoing tuning of balance between speed, cost, and accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。更高的缓存替换率提升了新鲜度，但可能增加延迟与成本，反映出在速度、成本与准确性之间的持续调参。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a3bb239fbc861bfa12617c1b184dec172d7c035ad6e01e9ca4c2a2d5448cf9f1/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a3bb239fbc861bfa12617c1b184dec172d7c035ad6e01e9ca4c2a2d5448cf9f1/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47719602' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eclecticlight.co/2026/04/10/why-you-cant-trust-privacy-security/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can&#x27;t trust macOS Privacy and Security settings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你不能完全信任 macOS 的隐私与安全设置</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 498</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS privacy and security settings can be misleading: several protections rely on OS-level implementations that apps can bypass or misreport. The piece outlines gaps in how permissions, telemetry, and kernel extensions are enforced and suggests practical hardening steps, including auditing app entitlements and minimizing third-party extensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 的隐私与安全设置并非全然可靠，其保护往往依赖于系统层面的实现，应用仍有绕过权限和数据收集的可能。文章指出存在的若干缺口，并给出具体做法，如审查应用授权、减少第三方扩展、避免依赖默认设定等。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://eclecticlight.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/insenticon.jpg' data-full='https://eclecticlight.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/insenticon.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c0rx7xzd10xo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cambodia unveils statue to honour famous landmine-sniffing rat</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>柬埔寨为著名排雷鼠揭幕雕像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47678573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47678573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cambodia unveiled a statue honoring Magawa, the famed mine-detection rat credited with saving lives through demining work. The tribute highlights animal contributions to humanitarian efforts and ongoing risk in post-conflict areas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>柬埔寨为著名排雷鼠 Magawa 揭幕雕像，以表彰其在排雷行动中挽救生命的功绩。该纪念体现了动物在人道主义行动中的重要作用，也凸显战后地区持续的安全风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/57be/live/78742bb0-308c-11f1-b297-95b0a0a8331e.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/57be/live/78742bb0-308c-11f1-b297-95b0a0a8331e.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
