<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.xda-developers.com/frances-government-ditching-windows-for-linux/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France&#x27;s government is ditching Windows for Linux, says US tech a strategic risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国政府放弃 Windows 转向 Linux，被视为对美方的战略风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47728653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 462</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47728653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France is moving its government desktop environment from Windows to Linux, citing sovereignty, security, and cost considerations. The move has become a flashpoint in debates about strategic tech dependencies, potentially reshaping public-sector procurement and software supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国政府将办公桌面系统从 Windows 转向 Linux，理由包括主权、安全与成本考量。这一举动引发关于对美方技术依赖的战略性争论，可能影响公共部门采购与软件供应链的韧性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thelettersfromleo.com/p/the-pentagon-threatened-pope-leo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Pentagon Threatened Pope Leo XIV&#x27;s Ambassador with the Avignon Papacy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五角大楼威胁阿维尼翁时期教皇使节</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47705952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 462</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47705952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative, era-spanning hypothetical sees the Pentagon pressuring a papal envoy during the Avignon Papacy era. The piece uses a historical lens to examine how power dynamics shape diplomacy across institutions, inviting reflection on modern geopolitics and institutional leverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个跨时代的设想情景，描绘五角大楼在阿维尼翁教皇时期对教皇使节施压。通过历史视角探讨权力如何影响跨机构的外交运作，引发对当代地缘政治与机构影响力的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!wLRo!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9558cb84-a466-4009-8945-ea577cd0443e_1536x1024.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!wLRo!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9558cb84-a466-4009-8945-ea577cd0443e_1536x1024.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/matthartman/ghost-pepper' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Ghost Pepper – Local hold-to-talk speech-to-text for macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Ghost Pepper——本地按住说话的语音转文本（macOS）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47666024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 459</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47666024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghost Pepper offers on-device speech-to-text for macOS, activated by a hold-to-talk gesture. By running locally, it preserves privacy and avoids cloud latency, appealing to developers building offline workflows or privacy-focused apps. This project could inspire tighter macOS integration for quick transcription without internet calls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghost Pepper 在 macOS 上实现本地语音转文本，按住说话即可转写。无需云端上传，强调隐私和离线使用，适合需要快速、低延迟转写的工作流。对想在系统层面集成转写功能的开发者来说，具有潜在的轻量化方向。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47727960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Pardonned.com – A searchable database of US Pardons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Pardonned.com – 美国赦免案件的可检索数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47727960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 453</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47727960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pardonned.com offers a searchable database of U.S. pardons, making an otherwise opaque area of legal history accessible to researchers, journalists, and the public. The project demonstrates how open data approaches can surface important records, with potential for API access and data exports to support analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pardonned.com 提供美国赦免案件的可检索数据库，方便研究者、记者与公众获取这类法律历史数据。该项目展示了开放数据在揭示重要记录方面的价值，同时具备 API 与数据导出以支持分析的潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Keychron/Keychron-Keyboards-Hardware-Design' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Industrial design files for Keychron keyboards and mice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Keychron 键盘和鼠标的工业设计档案公开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47720419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 449</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47720419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Keychron has open-sourced industrial design files for its keyboards and mice, inviting DIY fabrication, replication, and community-driven hardware tinkering. The move lowers barrier to modification, audits, and transparency, while highlighting how hardware design data can accelerate innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Keychron 公开了键盘和鼠标的工业设计档案，降低了自制、改装和硬件透明度的门槛。此举推动开源硬件社区的发展，同时也让设计数据更易于审计与创新。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47660286' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://belief.horse/notes/what-being-ripped-off-taught-me/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What being ripped off taught me</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>被坑教会了我什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 446</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal experience of being ripped off highlights the value of due diligence, clearer contracts, and consumer literacy. It underscores how hidden terms and misaligned incentives can blow up budgets, stressing preparation and skepticism for both individuals and startups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这段被坑的经历凸显尽职调查、清晰合约与消费者素养的重要性。隐藏条款与错配激励往往让预算失控，提醒个人和创业者在前期就要做好准备并保持警惕。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/openai-backs-bill-exempt-ai-firms-model-harm-lawsuits/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI backs Illinois bill that would limit when AI labs can be held liable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 支持伊利诺伊州提出的限制 AI 实验室承担责任的法案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47717587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 443</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47717587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 321</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI backs an Illinois bill that would limit when AI labs can be sued for harms their models cause. The measure seeks to carve out liability protections in certain scenarios, signaling a push to shield developers and influence how AI accountability is framed in the U.S.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 支持一项在伊利诺伊州提出的法案，该法案将限制 AI 实验室在某些情形下对其模型造成的伤害承担法律责任。提案旨在为开发者提供免责空间，并影响美国对 AI 问责框架的走向。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI chat assistant Claude reportedly swapped attribution of quotes, highlighting a flaw in accuracy and sourcing. It emphasizes the need for robust provenance checks, verifiable sources, and better model training to prevent mix-ups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对话模型混淆了不同人物的发言归属，暴露了 AI 在引用出处上的不足。此类错误可能传播错误信息，影响新闻、教育与决策场景，凸显对可验证来源与溯源机制的需求，以及改进训练数据和评估的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cr71lkzv49po' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chimpanzees in Uganda locked in eight-year &#x27;civil war&#x27;, say researchers</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47722333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 427</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47722333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report long-running coalitions and violence among chimpanzee communities in Uganda, describing it as an eight-year &#x27;civil war.&#x27; The findings shed light on primate social dynamics, territory disputes, and conservation challenges in human-influenced habitats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者指出乌干达黑猩猩群体存在八年之久的领地争夺与暴力冲突，类似‘内战’的局面。研究揭示灵长类社会结构、资源竞争以及在人类活动密集区域的保护挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kalmanfilter.net' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding the Kalman filter with a simple radar example</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用一个简单雷达案例理解卡尔曼滤波</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47693153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47693153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An accessible walkthrough of the Kalman filter using a simple radar example to illustrate the prediction-correction loop and how noisy measurements are fused into a real-time state estimate. The piece ties the method to practical applications in navigation, robotics, and sensor fusion, where uncertainty matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过一个简单雷达示例，直观讲解卡尔曼滤波的预测与校正过程，以及如何将带噪声的观测融合成实时状态估计。内容连接到了导航、机器人和传感器融合等实际应用场景，强调不确定性建模的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://werwolv.net/posts/usb_for_sw_devs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>USB for Software Developers: An introduction to writing userspace USB drivers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向软件开发者的 USB：用户态 USB 驱动入门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47695012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 401</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47695012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces the fundamentals of writing USB drivers that run in user space, contrasting them with kernel drivers and outlining common libraries and safety considerations. The piece provides practical steps and a small example to help developers prototype USB interactions quickly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文介绍在用户空间编写 USB 驱动的基础知识，比较了用户态驱动与内核驱动的差异，讨论常用库与安全注意事项，并给出一个实用的示例实现步骤。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ai.georgeliu.com/p/running-google-gemma-4-locally-with' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running Gemma 4 locally with LM Studio&#x27;s new headless CLI and Claude Code</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two tools enable running Gemma 4 locally: a headless CLI from LM Studio and Claude Code integration. This supports offline experimentation, privacy, and potential cost savings compared with cloud-hosted inference, signaling a trend toward local LLM deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LM Studio 的无头命令行工具与 Claude Code 集成使 Gemma 4 能在本地运行，支持离线实验、隐私保护以及相较云端推理的潜在成本节省。标志着对本地化大模型部署的持续推进。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/04/10/cpuid_site_hijacked/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CPU-Z and HWMonitor compromised</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CPU-Z 和 HWMonitor 被入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47717847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47717847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CPUID&#x27;s download infrastructure was hijacked to serve malware, illustrating how supply-chain and mirror compromises can affect trusted tooling. The incident reinforces the need for verified signatures, diverse mirrors, and user vigilance when installing widely-used utilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CPUID 的下载基础设施被入侵，伪装成恶意软件的镜像分发，暴露供应链和镜像源的风险。事件提醒用户核对签名、使用多源镜像并保持警觉。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2021/04/13/malware.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2021/04/13/malware.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://colaptop.pages.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Old laptops in a colo as low cost servers</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47707477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 392</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47707477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Old laptops in colo as low-cost servers shows that repurposing decommissioned laptops into colocation-hosted services can be a frugal alternative for hobby projects or small workloads, with caveats about reliability, power, and maintenance. Practical tips for making it workable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将退役笔记本放入 Colo 作为低成本服务器的做法，展示了以更低成本托管小型工作负载的途径，同时需关注可靠性、耗电与维护等挑战。给出使其可行的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ai.meta.com/blog/introducing-muse-spark-msl/?_fb_noscript=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse Spark: Scaling towards personal superintelligence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse Spark：向个人超级智能的规模化迈进</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47692043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 390</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47692043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 367</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Outlines a framework for scaling AI toward personal-scale intelligence, emphasizing privacy, personalized context, and smarter on-device capabilities. The initiative signals a push to blend private data with scalable models for more capable, private assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述将 AI 扩展到个人级别智能的框架，强调隐私、个性化上下文以及更智能的本地能力。这一举措旨在将私有数据与可扩展模型结合，打造更强大、更加私密的助理。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/04/10/south_korea_data_access_universal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>South Korea introduces universal basic mobile data access</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>韩国推出全民基本移动数据接入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47730407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 390</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47730407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>South Korea announces universal basic mobile data access, ensuring baseline data for all citizens. The policy aims to close the digital divide and spur digital services, while telecoms and regulators work out subsidies, network capacity, and privacy safeguards as it scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>韩国宣布全民基本移动数据接入，确保所有公民拥有基础数据。该政策旨在缩小数字鸿沟、促进数字服务，同时在扩大规模的过程中需要解决补贴、网络容量与隐私保护等挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/love2d/love' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LÖVE: 2D Game Framework for Lua</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47637377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 390</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47637377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LÖVE is a mature, open-source framework for building 2D games with Lua. It emphasizes simplicity and portability, with broad cross-platform support that makes it popular among indie developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LÖVE 是一个成熟的开源 Lua 2D 游戏框架，强调简单性和可移植性，得到广泛的跨平台支持，深受独立开发者欢迎。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lzon.ca/posts/other/microsoft-user-abuse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft is employing dark patterns to goad users into paying for storage?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软在使用暗性设计来诱导用户为存储付费？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Details how Microsoft allegedly uses UI patterns to push users toward paying for extra storage, raising questions about user trust and platform UX. The post urges awareness of such tactics and discusses implications for data ownership and billing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示微软据称使用 UI 策略推动用户为额外存储付费的做法，引发对用户信任与平台用户体验的关注。文章讨论此类策略的潜在影响，以及对数据拥有权与账单透明度的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47684326' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://slate.com/technology/2019/02/openai-gpt2-text-generating-algorithm-ai-dangerous.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI says its new model GPT-2 is too dangerous to release (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 称其新模型 GPT-2 太危险，无法公开发布（2019）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47684326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47684326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI chose not to release the full GPT-2 model due to potential misuse, releasing smaller versions and collaborating on safety research instead. The decision highlighted the tension between openness and preventing harm in AI, foreshadowing later policy debates about larger models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 因担忧被滥用而未发布 GPT-2 全量模型，只推出较小版本并进行安全研究合作。此举凸显了 AI 领域在开放与防范风险之间的权衡，也为后续对更大模型的政策讨论埋下伏笔。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://compote.slate.com/images/f55e2b1d-d608-44b9-93ac-9da6f1037598.jpeg?width=1560&amp;rect=1560x1040&amp;offset=0x0' data-full='https://compote.slate.com/images/f55e2b1d-d608-44b9-93ac-9da6f1037598.jpeg?width=1560&amp;rect=1560x1040&amp;offset=0x0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47649117' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/04/someone-at-browserstack-is-leaking-users-email-address/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Someone at BrowserStack is leaking users&#x27; email addresses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BrowserStack 内部人员泄露用户邮箱地址</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47649117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47649117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A BrowserStack employee has been leaking users&#x27; email addresses, causing a data exposure. This incident highlights the risk of insider threats and the need for strict access controls, auditing, and rapid response in SaaS platforms handling PII. Users should watch for follow-up guidance and any recommended account-related actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BrowserStack 一名内部人员泄露了用户的邮箱地址，造成数据暴露。此事件凸显了内部威胁的风险，以及对具备最小特权、审计和快速应对能力的访问控制的需求。用户应关注后续通知并采取必要的账户安全措施。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47649113' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/full/10.1080/23328940.2026.2645467#abstract' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finnish sauna heat exposure induces stronger immune cell than cytokine responses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>芬兰桑拿高温暴露激发更强的免疫细胞反应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47649113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 383</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47649113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 279</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Finnish sauna study shows that heat exposure from regular sauna use boosts circulating immune cells more than cytokine responses. The finding suggests sauna sessions may transiently strengthen certain immune defenses, with potential implications for immune health and infection resistance. However, results are early and should be weighed against other lifestyle factors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>芬兰桑拿高温暴露被发现能激活更多的免疫细胞，超出细胞因子反应。该结论暗示桑拿对免疫防御具有短暂的强化作用，或对健康管理有一定启示。研究尚处初步阶段，需结合其他生活方式因素综合评估。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47696035' data-tags='Legal,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thedrive.com/news/john-deere-to-pay-99-million-in-monumental-right-to-repair-settlement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John Deere to pay $99M in right-to-repair settlement</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47696035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47696035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A landmark right-to-repair settlement requiring Deere to pay $99 million and to improve access to tools and parts for repairs. The decision could influence OEM practices and consumer rights in agriculture and beyond.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一起具有里程碑意义的和解，要求 Deere 支付 9900 万美元并改善修理工具、零件及服务的获取，支持修理权。此举可能影响原厂对外提供维修能力的政策，对农业及其他领域的消费者权利具有示范效应。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare outlines a path to post-quantum security by 2029, with phased deployment and testing. The planning reflects industry urgency around quantum threats and crypto agility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 提出到 2029 年实现全面后量子安全的路线图，包含分阶段部署与测试。这反映出业界对量子威胁与加密灵活性的紧迫关注。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/a-truck-drive-spent-20-years-making-this-astonishing-scale-model-of-every-single-building-in-new-york-city-180988443/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A truck driver spent 20 years making a scale model of every building in NYC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一名卡车司机花20年制作纽约市所有建筑的缩尺模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47657268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 376</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47657268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A truck driver spent two decades building a precise scale model of every building in New York City, turning a lifelong hobby into a remarkable urban panorama. The project blends craftsmanship with data-driven representation, offering a personal, tangible record of a living city.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名卡车司机花了20年时间，逐座复制纽约市的所有建筑，做成精密的缩尺模型。这项手工与数据结合的工程，成为对这座活城的一部独特、可触及的记录。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Lw4W9V57SKs&amp;t=5716s' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Revision Demoparty 2026: Razor1911 [video]</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Razor1911 showcases at Revision Demoparty 2026, captured in a new video. The clips highlight the demoscene’s latest tricks—tight real-time graphics, music synchronization, and compact code tricks—reflecting how modern demos push hardware limits. For readers tracking real-time rendering and compression techniques, this offers a snapshot of current craftsmanship and community energy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Razor1911 在 2026 年 Revision Demoparty 的表演以新视频呈现，展现了高密度的实时图形、音乐同步和极简代码的技巧。片段体现了当代 demoscene 的制作工序与创新能力，以及对硬件极限的持续挑战。对关注实时渲染、编译器优化和边缘计算的技术读者来说，这是一组有启发性的案例。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47680404' data-tags='Data,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.allthingsdistributed.com/2026/04/s3-files-and-the-changing-face-of-s3.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>S3 Files</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>S3 文件与 S3 的新面貌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47680404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 371</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47680404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloud storage remains dominated by S3, but the service is evolving beyond a pure object store. The piece surveys new file-like semantics, tiering options, and API changes that blur the line between object storage and traditional file systems. These shifts have practical implications for developers and operators weighing cost, performance, and data management strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>S3 依然主导云存储，但服务正在向更丰富的文件化语义、分层存储和跨区域能力发展，模糊了对象存储与传统文件系统的边界。这些变化给开发者和运维带来更灵活的存取模式，同时也带来成本与性能权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.allthingsdistributed.com/images/sunflowers.jpg' data-full='https://www.allthingsdistributed.com/images/sunflowers.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Suzierizzo1/status/2040864617467924865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>81yo Dodgers fan can no longer get tickets because he doesn&#x27;t have a smartphone</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 367</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This incident highlights the move to digital-only ticketing and its accessibility gaps for seniors and non-smartphone users. It raises questions about inclusivity, and the need for alternative channels such as phone or in-person options for events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此事凸显数字化票务对老年人和非智能手机用户的无障碍挑战，促使人们思考包容性以及票务系统是否应保留电话或现场购票等替代渠道。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.construction-physics.com/p/helium-is-hard-to-replace' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Helium is hard to replace</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>氦气很难替代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 261</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Helium&#x27;s unique properties and global supply constraints mean finding drop-in replacements is nontrivial for tech manufacturing and cryogenics. The piece surveys why alternatives either underperform or introduce new trade-offs, stressing the need for long-term helium stewardship and diversification of suppliers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>氦气的独特性质及全球供应限制使得替代品难以直接替代，尤其在冷却与制造领域。文章梳理了可选替代方案的局限性与权衡，强调长期氦气管理与供应多元化的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/microsoft/microsoft-suspends-dev-accounts-for-high-profile-open-source-projects/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft suspends dev accounts for high-profile open source projects</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软暂停对知名开源项目的开发者账户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 364</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft suspends developer accounts tied to high-profile open-source projects, raising questions about platform governance and the impact on OSS communities. The move underscores ongoing tensions between commercial platform rules and open collaboration, with potential spillover for maintainers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软暂停与知名开源项目相关的开发者账户，引发对平台治理及对开源社区影响的讨论。这一举动凸显了商业平台规则与开源协作之间的紧张关系，可能对维护者产生连锁影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2025/08/07/Microsoft.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2025/08/07/Microsoft.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsky.app/profile/serenityforge.com/post/3mj3r4nbiws2t' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google removes &quot;Doki Doki Literature Club&quot; from Google Play</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google removed the game from Play Store, triggering questions about platform moderation. The move highlights how policy decisions affect indie games&#x27; visibility and monetization. It also sparks debate over criteria, consistency, and censorship on major app stores.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌已将该游戏从应用商店下架，引发关于平台审核与独立游戏曝光之间平衡的讨论。此举凸显了政策决策对独立开发者的可见度与盈利的影响，并引发关于标准、执行一致性以及平台审查尺度的辩论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47699181' data-tags='Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://astral.sh/blog/open-source-security-at-astral' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open source security at Astral</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Astral 的开源安全实践</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astral outlines its open-source security program, detailing how it tracks dependencies, audits code, and handles vulnerability remediation. It emphasizes responsible disclosure and cross-team collaboration to reduce supply-chain risk. The post offers a concrete blueprint for securing OSS within a product-driven company.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Astral 介绍其开源安全计划，覆盖依赖项审计、代码审查和漏洞修复流程，并强调负责任披露与跨团队协作以降低供应链风险。文中还提供在以产品为驱动的公司中落地开源安全的具体方案。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>志愿者将大约 1 万场演唱会的粉丝录音整理并上传至线上档案，扩大了现场演出的可访问性。此举体现了众包保存的力量，但也带来版权与许可在用户生成档案中的问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.osnews.com/story/144737/adobe-secretly-modifies-your-hosts-file-for-the-stupidest-reason/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Adobe modifies hosts file to detect whether Creative Cloud is installed</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adobe secretly modifies the hosts file to detect whether Creative Cloud is installed, a move that raises privacy and system-control concerns. The episode underscores how vendor anti-piracy or environment checks can affect users&#x27; control and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Adobe 偷偷修改了 hosts 文件以检测是否安装了 Creative Cloud，这一举动引发隐私与系统控制方面的担忧。事件凸显厂商的反盗版或环境检测如何影响用户的控制权与安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/04/germany-doxes-unkn-head-of-ru-ransomware-gangs-revil-gandcrab/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>German police name alleged leaders of GandCrab and REvil ransomware groups</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国警方公布GandCrab与REvil勒索软件团伙头目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 320</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>German authorities have publicly identified alleged leaders of the GandCrab and REvil ransomware groups, marking a high-profile intel disclosure in the ongoing cybercrime crackdown. Naming suspects can shift attribution and deter affiliates, yet it also raises questions about the evidence and legal process across borders. The move highlights how law enforcement is increasingly targeting leadership to disrupt ransomware ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国警方公开指认了GandCrab与REvil勒索软件团伙的疑似头目，成为对付网络犯罪的重大公开行动。点名头目有助于打击归因与阻断链条，但同时也引发关于跨境证据与法律程序的质疑。这一举动凸显执法部门正逐步以 leadership 打击勒索软件生态系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://krebsonsecurity.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/shchukin-kravchuk.png' data-full='https://krebsonsecurity.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/shchukin-kravchuk.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.gitbutler.com/series-a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;ve raised $17M to build what comes after Git</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>融资 1700 万美元，打造超越 Git 的下一代工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47712656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47712656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 696</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Git Butler raises $17M in Series A to build the next generation of version-control and collaboration tooling beyond Git, aiming to address collaboration bottlenecks, UX gaps, and scale for large teams. The funding signals continued investor interest in reimagining source control for modern workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>融资 1700 万美元，团队计划打造超越 Git 的下一代版本控制与协作工具，瞄准解决协作瓶颈、用户体验差距以及大团队的扩展性。此次融资表明投资者对重新定义现代工作流程的源代码管理工具仍然充满兴趣。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitbutler-docs-images-public.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/17-series-a.webp' data-full='https://gitbutler-docs-images-public.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/17-series-a.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.freestyle.sh/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Freestyle – Sandboxes for Coding Agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN：Freestyle——为编码代理提供沙箱环境</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47663147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47663147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Freestyle provides isolated sandboxes for running coding agents, enabling experimentation with AI-powered code agents without risking host systems. It aims to standardize an execution environment with safety boundaries, potentially accelerating research and deployment of autonomous coding assistants. If you work with agent-based automation, it’s worth watching.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Freestyle 提供用于编码代理的隔离沙箱，使在不危及主机系统的情况下测试和开发变得更安全。它试图为代码代理的执行建立统一的环境边界，可能加速自主编码助手的研究与落地。对于从事代理型自动化的开发者而言值得关注。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MegaTrain: Full Precision Training of 100B+ Parameter LLMs on a Single GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MegaTrain：在单个GPU上对1000亿以上参数的LLM进行全精度训练</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers claim to enable full-precision training of models with 100B+ parameters on a single GPU, likely via novel memory management and offload strategies. If real, this could lower the hardware barrier to ultra-large models, but raises questions about training speed, energy use, and practicality in real workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项研究声称可在单个GPU上对1000亿以上参数的模型进行全精度训练，可能依赖创新的内存管理与数据分流。若属实，将降低超大模型的硬件门槛，但也引发训练速度、能耗与实际可行性的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Charcuterie visualizes Unicode characters with similar shapes to help identify homoglyphs and phishing risks. This explorer makes it easier for developers and security researchers to spot look-alike symbols across fonts and languages, reducing the chance of spoofing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Charcuterie 将形状相似的 Unicode 字符以可视方式呈现，帮助识别同形字符及钓鱼风险。该工具适合开发者和安全研究者快速定位跨字体和语言的易混淆符号，从而降低欺骗风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://2swap.github.io/WeakC4/explanation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Optimal Strategy for Connect 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Connect 4 的最优策略</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47695213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 304</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47695213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of the exact winning strategy for Connect 4 under perfect play. By enumerating all positions and using retrograde analysis, the piece shows the game’s complete solution and offer takeaways for building game-playing AI and understanding search complexity in simple systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该分析给出 Connect 4 在完美对局下的完全解，通过穷举与回溯分析确立必胜路线。为构建棋类 AI 与理解简单系统的搜索复杂性提供可操作的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://2swap.github.io/WeakC4/sample.png' data-full='https://2swap.github.io/WeakC4/sample.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gadgetreview.com/maine-is-about-to-become-the-first-state-to-ban-major-new-data-centers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maine is about to become the first state to ban major new data centers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>缅因州将成为首个禁止新大型数据中心的州</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Maine moves to curb new large data centers, citing energy and grid impact. The policy could reshape regional tech investment, cloud footprint, and local power infrastructure while prompting debates over economic growth versus energy resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>缅因州拟通过政策限制新建大型数据中心，强调对能源与电网的影响。此举可能重塑区域科技投资、云计算布局及本地电力基础设施，并引发关于经济增长与能源韧性之间的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/2a8e079e-10a6-4599-9e6e-ddb65aa7f05b_1920x1080.jpg' data-full='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/2a8e079e-10a6-4599-9e6e-ddb65aa7f05b_1920x1080.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.unfolder.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unfolder for Mac – A 3D model unfolding tool for creating papercraft</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Unfolder for Mac：用于制作纸模型的 3D 展开工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unfolder for Mac converts 3D models into 2D unfoldings suitable for papercraft, enabling precise templates for printable sheets. It streamlines turning digital models into physical crafts, appealing to hobbyists, educators, and designers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Unfolder for Mac 将 3D 模型展开为适合纸艺的 2D 展开图，生成可打印的模板。它简化了将数字模型转化为实体手工艺品的过程，适合爱好者、教育工作者和设计师使用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47659109' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tboteproject.com/surveillancefindings/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Age verification as mass surveillance infrastructure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年龄验证成为大规模监控基础设施</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47659109' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47659109' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Age verification efforts are being deployed at scale, effectively creating a mass surveillance layer across digital platforms. The architecture often requires collecting personal data and behavior signals, raising serious privacy and governance concerns. Experts warn that such infrastructure could normalize pervasive tracking across services, with limited transparency about data use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>年龄验证正在被大规模部署，实际上成为一种大规模监控层。此类体系往往需要收集个人数据和行为信息，引发隐私和治理方面的严重担忧。专家警告此类基础设施可能让对数据使用透明度不足的情况下实现持续跟踪。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47656501' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/exQUIZitely/status/2040777977521398151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 1987 game “The Last Ninja” was 40 kilobytes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1987 年的游戏《The Last Ninja》仅用 40 KB 大小</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656501' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656501' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 186</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Last Ninja from 1987 proved a top-tier action experience could fit into just 40 kilobytes, thanks to clever compression, tile work, and optimization. It benchmarks how extreme efficiency can trump feature bloat in early software. The takeaway is a reminder of innovation under tight constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1987 年的《The Last Ninja》仅用 40 KB 就实现了高水平的动作体验，依靠巧妙的压缩、图块处理和代码优化。它展示了在严格约束下，极致效率如何胜过功能臃肿。核心启示是对早期软件创新在资源极限下的反思。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2040577709139468288/img/neVMuuEJWI0iTV_L.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2040577709139468288/img/neVMuuEJWI0iTV_L.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://boringbar.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: boringBar – a taskbar-style dock replacement for macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：boringBar——macOS 的任务栏风格 Dock 替代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>boringBar is a macOS replacement for the Dock with a taskbar-style interface. It emphasizes persistent pinned apps, quick-launch, and consistent shortcuts across spaces, offering an alternative UX to Apple’s native Dock. If it gains traction, it signals demand for more modular, multitasking-oriented macOS UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>boringBar 是一个为 macOS 设计的 Dock 替代，采用类似任务栏的界面，便于固定应用、快速启动并在各工作区保持一致。它为多任务工作提供了新的交互方式，若受欢迎，可能反映出对更模块化、以多任务为导向的 macOS UI 的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://boringbar.app/images/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://boringbar.app/images/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A candid reflective post about monetization and authenticity, exploring what it means to &#x27;sell out&#x27; in a creator&#x27;s career. It offers lessons on balancing sustainability with staying true to craft and audience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者就商业化与初心之间的张力进行了坦诚反思，讨论在创作者生涯中“卖不卖”到底意味着什么。文章分享在实现可持续发展与坚持创作初衷、对观众的忠诚之间的取舍与经验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/jTGbKKGqs5EdyYoRc/most-people-can-t-juggle-one-ball' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most people can&#x27;t juggle one ball</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多数人都难以同时完成一个任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47702887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 293</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47702887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues cognitive limits cap how many things we can track or manage; multitasking often reduces performance even with simple tasks. This finding has implications for UX and product design, reminding designers not to promise effortless multitasking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张人类在同时处理多项任务时存在认知极限，哪怕是处理一个简单任务也会有困难。这一发现对用户体验和产品设计有启示：避免把多任务承诺得过于美好，需更真实地考虑人类注意力与工作记忆的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/v1654295382/new_mississippi_river_fjdmww.jpg' data-full='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/v1654295382/new_mississippi_river_fjdmww.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theamericanscholar.org/the-importance-of-being-idle/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Importance of Being Idle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>闲暇的重要性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47666639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 290</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47666639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that idle time fuels creativity, reflection, and long-term learning, challenging hustle culture. It blends anecdotes and research to show downtime can sharpen focus when you return to work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章阐述空闲时间能促进创造力、反思和长期学习，挑战过度忙碌的工作文化。通过实例与研究，展示休息在回到工作时能让注意力变得更集中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theamericanscholar.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/zaretsky-lafargue-1.jpg' data-full='https://theamericanscholar.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/zaretsky-lafargue-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece discusses how a cryptic yet compelling title can trigger curiosity and action, offering takeaways for creators designing hooks and headlines. It blends psychology with practical guidance on clarity, surface-level intrigue, and setting reader expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论如何用隐秘却足以促使行动的标题吸引读者，结合认知心理与实用建议，帮助创作者设计更具引导力的标题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/callumlocke/json-formatter' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JSON formatter Chrome plugin now closed and injecting adware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome JSON Formatter 插件现已关闭，且被注入广告软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47721946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47721946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Chrome extension formerly known as JSON Formatter has been shut down after revelations that it injected adware into users&#x27; browsers. This underscores ongoing security risks in browser extensions and the need for careful vetting of third-party tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome JSON Formatter 插件已被关闭，同时被证实注入广告软件，影响用户浏览体验。事件凸显浏览器扩展的安全风险，强调对第三方工具的审查与权限控制。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The book probes how information and memetics intersect with security culture, using a fictional premise to challenge readers on how organizations handle hidden information and cognitive biases. It offers takeaways about trust, verification, and the limits of memetic reasoning in tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本书以虚构设定探讨信息与模因在安全文化中的交汇，挑战读者对组织如何处理隐性信息与认知偏差的理解。它就信任、验证以及在科技领域对模因式推理的局限性提出了启示。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple pushed a software update that remedies a passcode bug that could lock users out of their devices. The fix helps restore access and reduces long-term risk of permanent lockouts. This incident underscores how critical small software bugs can be on everyday devices and the value of timely patches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果发布软件更新修复了一个可能导致用户被锁定在设备之外的密码错误漏洞。修复让用户重新获得访问权限，降低长期被永久锁定的风险。此事凸显了日常设备上微小漏洞的影响以及及时修补的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advanced Mac Substitute reimplements classic Mac OS APIs at the API level, enabling modern code to run or simulate 1980s software environments. It showcases retro-computing work that preserves legacy software and provides insight into OS API evolution.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netflix price hikes spark a rethink of streaming value; the author buys a DVD player to own physical media, illustrating how price sensitivity and ownership influence media consumption choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Netflix 价格上涨让人重新审视流媒体的价值，作者改买 DVD 播放机以获得对媒体的拥有权，体现了价格敏感性与所有权对消费选择的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本入门介绍讲解肽的定义、在生物医学中的重要性，以及初学者应该关注的基础知识。文章给出入门路径、常见研究方向和可用的资源，帮助读者负责任地开启肽领域的探索。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47733561' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zenodo.org/records/19513269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>447 TB/cm² at zero retention energy – atomic-scale memory on fluorographane</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在氟碳烷上的原子级存储：447 TB/cm² 且零保持能量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports a record atomic-scale memory density of 447 terabits per square centimeter in fluorographane without retention energy required. This points to potential leaps in future memory technology, though challenges remain for practical integration and room-temperature operation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道在氟碳烷材料上实现原子级存储密度，达到每平方厘米 447 Tb 的记录，且不需要保持能量。此成果预示未来存储技术的突破潜力，但在实际集成与室温工作方面仍存在挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zenodo.org/static/images/invenio-rdm.svg' data-full='https://zenodo.org/static/images/invenio-rdm.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theintercept.com/2026/04/09/trump-documents-library-presidential-records-act/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DOJ wants to scrap Watergate-era rule that makes presidential records public</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>司法部拟废除水门时期的总统档案公开规则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47722566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47722566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The DOJ wants to scrap a Nixon-era rule that makes presidential records public, a move that could shrink archival transparency. If enacted, the change could alter how quickly and widely past administrations&#x27; documents are released and scrutinized.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>司法部拟废除水门时期的一项规则，该规则确保总统档案公开。这一变动可能削弱档案的透明度与公众访问权，并影响历任政府记录的披露速度与范围。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-1258566797-e1775672169422.jpg?fit=3000%2C1500&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' data-full='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-1258566797-e1775672169422.jpg?fit=3000%2C1500&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://moonrf.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An open-source 240-antenna array to bounce signals off the Moon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源的 240 天线阵列，可将信号反射至月球</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source project builds a 240-antenna array designed to bounce signals off the Moon, enabling distributed lunar communications experiments. The setup showcases collaborative, crowd-sourced hardware for space research and amateur radio exploration. It demonstrates how open designs can accelerate low-cost, high-pidelity deep-space work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个开源项目，构建了一个由 240 根天线组成的阵列，用以把信号反射到月球，支持分布式月球通信实验。该方案体现了协作、众包硬件在太空研究和业余无线电探索中的潜力，展示了开源设计如何推动低成本的高保真深空工作。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特朗普警告若伊朗不达成协议，整个人类文明今晚将死去，凸显核谈判中的高风险态势。这番言论反映地缘政治紧张局势可能以市场、安全与科技政策等多种渠道产生影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI-generated singer now claims eleven spots on iTunes singles charts, underscoring how synthetic voices can compete with human performers in mainstream music. It signals a shift in digital music economics and rights management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位AI生成的歌手已在 iTunes 单曲榜占据11个席位，凸显合成声音可以进入主流音乐市场。此趋势引发关于署名、授权与声音艺术未来的讨论，即便粉丝购买与收听。反映出数字音乐经济与版权管理的变革。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notes.brooklynzelenka.com/Blog/Surelock' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Surelock: Deadlock-Free Mutexes for Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Surelock：Rust 的无死锁互斥锁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47693559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47693559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Surelock presents deadlock-free mutexes for Rust, reducing a common source of concurrency bugs. By enforcing safer lock acquisition patterns, it helps developers build more reliable multi-threaded code, though it may require changing how you design synchronization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Surelock 为 Rust 提供无死锁的互斥锁原语，降低并发编程中常见的死锁风险。通过强制更安全的锁获取策略，帮助开发者构建更可靠的多线程代码，但在设计同步时可能需要调整做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://notes.brooklynzelenka.com/Blog/Surelock-og-image.webp' data-full='https://notes.brooklynzelenka.com/Blog/Surelock-og-image.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.coindesk.com/markets/2026/03/22/bitcoin-miners-are-losing-usd19-000-on-every-btc-produced-as-difficulty-drops-7-8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bitcoin miners are losing on every coin produced as difficulty drops</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比特币矿工在难度下降时每开采一枚币都在亏损</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47730370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47730370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Miners are losing about $19,000 per BTC mined as difficulty falls, squeezing margins for operators with fixed costs. The economics shift: lower difficulty reduces revenue more than it reduces operating costs, prompting questions about miner profitability and upside for different business models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着难度下降，比特币矿工在每开采一枚BTC时亏损约1.9万美元，挤压了固定成本结构下的利润。难度下降带来收入下降的速度快于成本下降，令矿工盈利前景与商业模式的可持续性成为关注点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/s3y3vcno/production/09c7af12c3ed9c083ddc433486f71cdad7b77bfd-2560x1416.jpg?auto=format&amp;w=960&amp;h=540&amp;crop=focalpoint&amp;fit=clip&amp;q=75&amp;fm=jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/s3y3vcno/production/09c7af12c3ed9c083ddc433486f71cdad7b77bfd-2560x1416.jpg?auto=format&amp;w=960&amp;h=540&amp;crop=focalpoint&amp;fit=clip&amp;q=75&amp;fm=jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.infoq.com/news/2026/04/google-agent-testbed-scion/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google open-sources experimental agent orchestration testbed Scion</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47675213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47675213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google open-sources Scion, an experimental agent orchestration testbed to study how autonomous agents coordinate tasks, manage safety constraints, and interact with tools. The project aims to accelerate research into reliable agent ecosystems and safe AI automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌开源了 Scion，一套用于实验的代理编排测试平台，研究自治代理如何协同、遵循安全约束并与工具交互。该项目意在加速对可靠代理生态和安全自动化的研究。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47713495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47713495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47677885' data-tags='Web,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://printervention.app/details' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rescuing old printers with an in-browser Linux VM bridged to WebUSB over USB/IP</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用浏览器中的 Linux 虚拟机，借助 WebUSB/USB/IP 拯救老印表机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47677885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47677885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project bridges old printers to modern machines by running a Linux VM inside the browser and exposing it via WebUSB/USB/IP, letting legacy hardware print without vendor drivers. The approach unlocks long-tail devices for web-enabled environments and showcases browser-based hardware emulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目通过在浏览器中运行 Linux 虚拟机并通过 WebUSB/USB/IP 暴露，连接并驱动老旧打印机，无需厂商驱动即可打印。这种在浏览器层实现的硬件仿真方法，为长期积压的设备重新获得可用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://printervention.app/cat.jpeg' data-full='https://printervention.app/cat.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://khronokernel.com/macos/2023/08/08/AS-VM.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Silicon and Virtual Machines: Beating the 2 VM Limit (2023)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows practical ways to run more than two virtual machines on Apple Silicon, along with the hardware and software caveats involved. The takeaway is that while feasible with certain hypervisor setups, performance and memory overhead become the main bottlenecks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示在 Apple Silicon 上运行多于两台虚拟机的实际做法，以及相关的硬件与软件限制。要点在于，借助特定的虚拟机/仿真工具可以实现，但性能和内存开销将成为主要瓶颈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://khronokernel.com/favicon.ico' data-full='https://khronokernel.com/favicon.ico' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47673541' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dornsife.usc.edu/news/stories/ai-may-be-making-us-think-and-write-more-alike/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI may be making us think and write more alike</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能可能让我们的思考与写作变得更趋于一致</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47673541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47673541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI assistance is correlated with more uniform thinking and writing styles among users, suggesting homogenization of ideas and prose.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，人工智能帮助下人们的思考和写作风格趋于一致，可能削弱表达多样性。该文章讨论教育、创造力和网络话语等领域在 AI 辅助日益普及背景下的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dornsife.usc.edu/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/7/2026/03/llm-homogenous-thought-top.jpg' data-full='https://dornsife.usc.edu/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/7/2026/03/llm-homogenous-thought-top.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
