<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mattmireles/gemma-tuner-multimodal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Gemma 4 Multimodal Fine-Tuner for Apple Silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemma 4：Apple Silicon 的多模态微调器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47680309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47680309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 is a multimodal model fine-tuner designed to run on Apple Silicon, enabling on-device training and fine-tuning without sending data to the cloud. It emphasizes practical on-device ML workflows, performance and privacy, opening up experiments for hobbyists and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 是为 Apple Silicon 设计的多模态模型微调工具，支持在设备端进行模型微调，无需将数据上传云端。它强调本地化的 ML 工作流、性能与隐私优势，为爱好者和研究者提供更便捷的实验途径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f874c81fd8702fd76c6c5d1df98d604006fd9645831ab0a7b1b83378acc07e1c/mattmireles/gemma-tuner-multimodal' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f874c81fd8702fd76c6c5d1df98d604006fd9645831ab0a7b1b83378acc07e1c/mattmireles/gemma-tuner-multimodal' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bigbrotherwatch.org.uk/blog/apples-new-iphone-update-is-restricting-internet-freedom-in-the-uk/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New iPhone age and identity checks restrict internet freedom in the UK</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新 iPhone 的年龄与身份检查限制英国互联网自由</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47712222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47712222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyzes Apple&#x27;s new iPhone update introducing age-verification and identity checks, arguing these measures curb online freedom and raise privacy and surveillance concerns. It situates the change within tech policy and rights debates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评估苹果最新 iPhone 更新中的年龄与身份验证措施，认为这些做法削弱了英国的网络自由，并引发隐私与监控等议题。将此变动置于科技政策与公民权利讨论中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bigbrotherwatch.org.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/IOS-age-check.jpg' data-full='https://bigbrotherwatch.org.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/IOS-age-check.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eaw.app/picoz80/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PicoZ80 – Drop-In Z80 Replacement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PicoZ80：可插拔的 Z80 替代处理器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PicoZ80 provides a drop-in, Z80-compatible CPU core for retro computing projects, enabling hobbyists to swap in a modern core without rewriting software. It aims to simplify hardware design and integration for emulators, FPGA builds, and educational tinkering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PicoZ80 提供一个与 Z80 兼容的、可直接替换的 CPU 核心，方便在复古计算项目中无须改写软件即可替换处理器。该项目旨在简化在仿真、FPGA 构建及教学实践中的硬件设计与集成。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hatchmag.com/articles/trump-administration-orders-dismantling-us-forest-service/7716263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump administration orders dismantling of the U.S. Forest Service</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普政府下令解散美国林务局</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47698132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47698132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An allegation that the Trump administration ordered dismantling the U.S. Forest Service has circulated, raising questions about the future of federal land management and wildfire response. If true, it would represent a sweeping reorganization with wide-ranging implications for conservation, federal staffing, and land-use data. The piece places that claim in the context of ongoing political battles over environmental policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有传言称特朗普政府下令解散美国林务局，涉及联邦林地管理与野火应对等议题。若为真，这将标志一次大规模的政府重组，影响环保、人员编制与林地数据等方面。文中将此事置于持续的环境政策政治博弈之中。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/44257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code login fails with OAuth timeout on Windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 在 Windows 上因 OAuth 超时而无法登录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An issue reports Claude Code login failing on Windows due to OAuth timeout, highlighting cross‑platform authentication challenges for AI copilots. Workarounds exist, and the incident underscores the need for robust, user-friendly sign-in flows across OSes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个问题报告指出在 Windows 上使用 Claude Code 时因 OAuth 超时而无法登录，暴露了跨平台认证的挑战。常见的解决方法存在，这次事件强调了在不同操作系统上提供稳健、易用的登录流程的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://steveblank.com/2026/04/09/nowhere-is-safe/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nowhere is safe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没有一个地方是安全的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47722562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47722562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 286</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steve Blank argues no system is truly secure in the modern threat landscape, urging stronger threat modeling and continuous testing. The piece emphasizes resilience over perfection in security practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者指出在现代威胁环境下没有系统能绝对安全，需要更强的威胁建模与持续演练来提升韧性。强调安全应是持续的治理与改进过程。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.veitheller.de/Generative_art_over_the_years.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Generative art over the years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生成式艺术的演变</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From algorithmic rule-based systems to neural networks, generative art has evolved with tools that democratize creation and expand creative possibilities; the piece traces historical milestones, techniques, and stylistic trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>生成式艺术从基于规则的算法发展到神经网络驱动，工具的普及让创作更具民主性，扩展了创意边界。文中梳理了历史里程碑、常用技术与风格演变。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/dotnet/csharp-15-union-types/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Union types in C# 15</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C# 15 中的联合类型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47645717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47645717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C# 15 adds union types to support discriminated unions and safer pattern matching. The change improves expressiveness for branching logic and can reduce boilerplate in data-heavy scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C# 15 引入联合类型，支持判别式联合与更安全的模式匹配。此改动提高了分支逻辑的表达能力，能在数据密集场景中减少样板代码。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After two decades using AdSense, the author stopped running ads on their sites as of 2025, sharing the rationale and observed trade-offs. The post touches on monetization, user experience, and the evolving ad ecosystem, offering lessons for solo developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在使用 AdSense 二十年后，作者于 2025 年停止在网站投放广告，分享了原因和观察到的权衡。该文探讨了变现、用户体验以及广告生态的演变，给独立开发者一些经验教训。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidoks.blog/p/how-funerals-keep-africa-poor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Many African families spend fortunes burying their dead</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Funeral costs drive financial hardship across many African families, leading to debt and asset depletion. The piece calls for affordable, scalable solutions—community funds, microinsurance, or policy interventions—to ease end-of-life expenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>葬礼高昂的费用在许多非洲家庭中造成经济负担，常导致债务累积和资产缩水。文章呼吁通过社区基金、微保等可负担、可扩展的解决方案，降低安葬成本压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!m4zD!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0417c22b-4a4f-40c2-8baa-118fb9ffb2f6_2362x1575.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!m4zD!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0417c22b-4a4f-40c2-8baa-118fb9ffb2f6_2362x1575.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.railway.com/p/moving-railways-frontend-off-nextjs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We moved Railway&#x27;s frontend off Next.js. Builds went from 10+ mins to under 2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Railway 前端从 Next.js 迁移，构建时间降至不足两分钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47685945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47685945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Railway migrated its frontend away from Next.js, slashing build times from 10+ minutes to under 2. This showcases how platform-level optimizations and tooling choices can drastically speed iteration and deployments in a SaaS product. The move likely involved shifting tooling, bundling, and deployment pipelines, offering a case study for engineering teams facing long build cycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Railway 将前端从 Next.js 迁出，构建时间从十多分钟降至不足两分钟。这一改动凸显了底层工具与构建管线的选择对开发效率的巨大影响。对于需要快速验证与迭代的 SaaS 团队，这提供了一个有价值的经验案例。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Eleventy 的终结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47735535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47735535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues Eleventy may be losing traction as modern front-end stacks demand more dynamic, integrated tooling. It highlights tradeoffs between simplicity and capability in static-site workflows, urging teams to re-evaluate their tooling choices as ecosystems evolve. The author suggests exploring hybrid approaches or newer frameworks that offer better scalability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为 Eleventy 在当今需要动态特性和集成工具的前端生态中可能逐渐失去吸引力。作者讨论静态站点工作流的取舍，建议团队在生态演进中重新评估工具选择，并考虑更具扩展性的混合方案或新框架。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.skypilot.co/research-driven-agents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Research-Driven Agents: When an agent reads before it codes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究驱动的代理：代理在编码前先阅读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for research-driven agents that read and verify documentation before writing code; discusses the benefits for reliability and safety, plus practical patterns for integrating reading steps into agent planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出“研究驱动的代理”想法，即在编码前先阅读并核验文档以提升可靠性和安全性，并讨论将阅读步骤融入代理计划的实用做法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001106-codex-rate-card' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex pricing to align with API token usage, instead of per-message</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex 定价将按 API 令牌使用量结算，而非按消息计费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47650726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47650726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codex pricing will align with API token usage, instead of per-message. This shift ties pricing to actual compute and token consumption, potentially changing costs for longer prompts or code generation. Developers should expect more predictable pricing and may adjust usage patterns accordingly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将 Codex 定价改为按 API 令牌使用量计费，而非按消息数量。此变动更直接地反映实际的计算成本，长提示和代码生成可能会因此改变成本结构。开发者应期待更可预测的按令牌计费，并据此调整使用习惯。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47736298' data-tags='Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/law/2026/apr/11/appeals-court-ruling-home-distilling-ban-unconstitutional' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US appeals court declares 158-year-old home distilling ban unconstitutional</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美上诉法院裁定158年历史的家庭蒸馏禁令违宪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A federal appeals court struck down a 158-year-old ban on home distilling, ruling it unconstitutional. The decision highlights how legacy regulations can linger in the code and invites reevaluation of other antique restrictions. For makers and hobbyists, it signals potential growth in DIY spirits while underscoring the need to navigate safety and taxation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦上诉法院裁定一项158年前的家庭蒸馏禁令违宪。裁决暴露出法规体系中的历史遗留条文，促使人们重新审视其他古旧限制的效力。对家庭蒸馏爱好者而言，这可能扩展DIY烈酒的空间，同时也需关注安全与税务合规。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/a859b633790bc8805cfd1c9fa2c9cf44ebdacb20/0_0_4074_3261/master/4074.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=9937f75b569c27761a6fdc548bf37e8b' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/a859b633790bc8805cfd1c9fa2c9cf44ebdacb20/0_0_4074_3261/master/4074.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=9937f75b569c27761a6fdc548bf37e8b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47738978' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogfontawesome.wpcomstaging.com/we-have-a-99-email-reputation-gmail-disagrees/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We have a 99% email reputation, but Gmail disagrees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们的邮件信誉高达99%，但 Gmail 不认同</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite claiming a 99% email reputation score from one service, Gmail disagrees, leading to confusion about deliverability. The incident highlights variability in reputation metrics and the importance of robust email authentication practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管某服务给出99%的邮件信誉分，但 Gmail 的判定并不认同，导致投递问题与误解。此事凸显邮件信誉评估之间的差异以及强化 DKIM/SPF 等认证、送达策略的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogfontawesome.wpcomstaging.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/email-frustration.png' data-full='https://blogfontawesome.wpcomstaging.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/email-frustration.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260408-the-extinct-english-words-for-just-the-two-of-us' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wit, unker, Git: The lost medieval pronouns of English intimacy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wit、Unker、Git：英语亲密关系的失落中世纪代词</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47701572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47701572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article uncovers rare medieval English pronouns used to denote intimacy, tracing how linguistic forms encode social closeness. It reveals how language shifts reflect cultural norms and how today’s pronoun choices echo centuries-old conventions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中揭示用于表达亲密关系的稀有中世纪英语代词，追踪语言形式如何编码社会亲近度。揭示语言演变与文化规范之间的关系，以及当代代词选择在某种程度上延续了数百年的传统。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Phyphox enables physical experiments using smartphone sensors, turning everyday devices into lab instruments. It lowers barriers for STEM education and fieldwork by offering ready-made experiments and data collection tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Phyphox 让智能手机内置传感器成为物理实验工具，降低教育与现场实验的门槛。通过现成的实验和数据采集工具，方便教师与学生进行动手研究。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LibreOffice 的基金会发布正式更新，结束外界对其未来路线的猜测。文中明确了下一步的计划与治理透明度，并重申对开源开发的长期承诺。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mikf/gallery-dl/discussions/9304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Media scraper Gallery-dl is moving to Codeberg after receiving a DMCA notice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gallery-dl 媒体抓取工具在收到 DMCA 通知后迁往 Codeberg</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gallery-dl, a popular media-scraping tool, is moving its repository to Codeberg after a DMCA takedown notice. This underscores how hosting choices can pressure OSS projects to relocate, and highlights Codeberg as an appealing alternative for projects seeking EU-based hosting and privacy-forward policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知名媒体抓取工具 Gallery-dl 在收到 DMCA 通知后将其代码库迁移至 Codeberg。这凸显开源项目在托管平台选择上的压力，以及 Codeberg 作为欧洲基地更注重隐私与合规性的潜在吸引力。</p>

It appeals to developers and sysadmins who want fast, keyboard-driven data work without GUI overhead. It highlights a growing niche of CLI-first productivity tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sheets 是一个基于终端的电子表格工具，强调简洁性和可脚本化的工作流，方便在命令行中快速处理数据。它受开发者与系统管理员欢迎，追求无 GUI 的高效键盘操作。凸显了日益兴起的 CLI 优先生产力工具领域。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical look at using SQLite in production from a storefront perspective: single-file DB, migrations, performance, and reliability lessons.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从餐饮/小型商店场景出发，分享在生产环境使用 SQLite 的经验教训：单文件数据库、迁移、性能与可靠性要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47650312' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mattkeeter.com/blog/2026-04-05-tailcall/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A tail-call interpreter in (nightly) Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 nightly Rust 中实现的尾调用解释器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47650312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47650312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tail-call interpreter implemented for Rust nightly, showcasing how tail-call optimization can simplify recursion-heavy interpreters. It offers a compact exploration of interpreter design in Rust with advanced nightly features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于 Rust nightly 实现的尾调用解释器，展示了在拥有严格所有权模型的语言中应用尾调用优化以简化递归解释器的思路。该作品为使用 Rust 构建解释器与 DSL 提供了简洁的设计示例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mattkeeter.com/blog/2026-04-05-tailcall/hero.png' data-full='https://mattkeeter.com/blog/2026-04-05-tailcall/hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47672941' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pijul.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pijul a FOSS distributed version control system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pijul：一个开源分布式版本控制系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pijul is an open-source distributed version control system grounded in patch-based history and a functional data model, offering a different approach from Git. It emphasizes composability and formal reasoning about changes, appealing to projects seeking alternative VCS semantics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pijul 是一个开源分布式版本控制系统，采用基于补丁的历史与函数式数据模型，与 Git 提供了不同的语义。它强调可组合性和对变更的形式化推理，适合寻求替代 VCS 语义的项目。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47731960' data-tags='Open Source,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.osnews.com/story/144776/the-disturbing-white-paper-red-hat-is-trying-to-erase-from-the-internet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The disturbing white paper Red Hat is trying to erase from the internet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Red Hat 正在从网上删除一份具争议性的白皮书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47731960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47731960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A controversial white paper is being erased by Red Hat, raising concerns about censorship and corporate influence over open-source discourse. The move spotlights how big players can shape publicly available knowledge and potentially suppress critical discussions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Red Hat 正在从网上删除一份具争议性的白皮书，引发关于审查与大厂对开源话语控制的担忧。此举暴露了大型企业对公开知识的影响力及可能压制重要讨论的风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47716809' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfarq.homeip.net/intel-486-cpu-announced-april-10-1989/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intel 486 CPU announced April 10, 1989</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔 80486 处理器于1989年4月10日宣布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 486 introduced on-chip cache and improved architecture, delivering a substantial speedup over 386 and cementing x86 dominance in PCs. This milestone accelerated desktop software development and hardware innovations for years.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>486 处理器在1989年4月宣布，带来片上缓存和架构改进，相较于 386 给予显著性能提升，巩固了 x86 在个人电脑中的主导地位。这一里程碑推动了桌面软件和硬件的长线发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/intel-486-social.jpg?fit=892%2C502&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/intel-486-social.jpg?fit=892%2C502&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47713584' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/c2evppm30p7o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Afrika Bambaataa has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>嘻哈先驱 Afrika Bambaataa 去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47713584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47713584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>One of hip-hop&#x27;s earliest pioneers has died, marking the end of an era in New York&#x27;s music scene. He helped spawn the Zulu Nation and played a pivotal role in propelling hip-hop from local clubs to a global cultural movement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>早期嘻哈奠基人去世，标志着纽约音乐场景一个时代的结束。他推动 Zulu Nation 的成立并帮助将嘻哈从本地夜店带向全球文化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/7483/live/0aeb9580-3477-11f1-a27c-25e679607533.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/7483/live/0aeb9580-3477-11f1-a27c-25e679607533.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47662116' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anzellai/sky' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sky – an Elm-inspired language that compiles to Go</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sky：Elm 启发的语言，编译为 Go</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sky is an Elm-inspired language that compiles to Go, aiming to bring safer, more ergonomic functional style to Go projects. By targeting Go’s ecosystem, it seeks practical interoperability and smoother state management while offering cleaner abstractions for UI and logic. It represents a thoughtful experiment at the intersection of FP ergonomics and Go&#x27;s pragmatism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sky 是一门受 Elm 启发、可编译为 Go 的语言，旨在为 Go 项目带来更安全、更高效的函数式风格。通过面向 Go 生态系统，它寻求实际的互操作性和更流畅的状态管理，同时提供更清晰的 UI 与逻辑抽象。它展示了在函数式编程的易用性与 Go 的务实之间的一个有趣实验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/19c7ae293ad8d082fdb9154fc674f9ef58f4fb64b4e5f95dbd0accd5907eaa27/anzellai/sky' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/19c7ae293ad8d082fdb9154fc674f9ef58f4fb64b4e5f95dbd0accd5907eaa27/anzellai/sky' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>计算物理学（第二版）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47650502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47650502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comprehensive textbook update on computational physics, covering numerical methods for simulating physical systems and analyzing data. The second edition adds modern techniques and practical exercises that bridge theory and computation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本计算物理的教材更新版系统地介绍用于物理系统数值模拟与数据分析的数值方法。修订版加入现代技术与实践练习，帮助理论与计算的结合。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JVM 选项探索器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A web-based explorer for Java Virtual Machine options helps developers discover and compare flags, from GC tuning to JIT and memory settings. It reduces guesswork during JVM tuning and speeds up performance experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个在线的 JVM 参数浏览器，方便开发者查找、对比各类参数及其用途，包括垃圾回收、JIT 和内存设置。能够降低调优中的试错成本，加速性能试验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tubesoundquiz.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Identify a London Underground Line just by listening to it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仅凭声音识别伦敦地铁线路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive project challenges you to identify which London Underground line is playing by the sonic cues alone. It showcases how brand­ing and infrastructure acoustics encode identity, offering a playful angle on urban sound design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个互动项目让玩家仅凭声音线索识别所播放的伦敦地铁线路，展示声音标识和基础设施声学如何编码城市身份，并带来关于城市声音设计的趣味视角。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47710845' data-tags='Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/webdev/comments/1sglytg/bunnycdn_has_been_silently_losing_our_production/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BunnyCDN has been silently losing our production files for 15 months</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BunnyCDN 已悄悄在过去 15 个月中丢失生产文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-running data-loss incident at BunnyCDN leaves customer production files missing for over a year, highlighting data-loss risk and the need for robust backups and transparency. The episode underscores the fragility of relying on third-party providers for critical data and the importance of incident transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BunnyCDN 长期数据丢失事件导致客户的生产文件在一年多的时间里丢失，暴露出数据丢失风险以及对稳健备份和透明度的需求。此事凸显依赖第三方存储对运营数据的潜在脆弱性，以及对事件透明度的迫切需要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47710466' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.kagi.com/tips/redirects' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kagi Product Tips – Customize Your Search Results with URL Redirects</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kagi 产品技巧——通过 URL 重定向自定义搜索结果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kagi shows how to tailor search results by using URL redirects, letting you steer queries to specific endpoints or domains. The approach enables targeted results, experiments, and domain-specific workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kagi 公布如何通过 URL 重定向定制搜索结果，让你将查询导向特定端点或域名，实现更精准的结果呈现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kagifeedback.org/assets/files/2026-04-06/1775499239-315386-cards2.png' data-full='https://kagifeedback.org/assets/files/2026-04-06/1775499239-315386-cards2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47742790' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tanyaverma.sh/2026/04/10/closing-of-the-frontier.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Closing of the Frontier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>前沿的收束：创新边界正在收缩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the era of easy, open-ended frontier exploration is ending as regulatory, geopolitical, and funding constraints tighten the boundaries. It discusses how this shift will reshape risk-taking, investment, and innovation strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，随着监管、地缘政治和资金约束加剧，曾经容易探索的前沿正在收缩。此变动将改变风险偏好、投资方向及创新策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A demo shows Oberon System 3 running natively on Raspberry Pi 3, accompanied by a ready-to-use SD card image. It demonstrates how a compact, single-tasking OS can still be useful for education and experimentation on modern SBCs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>演示展示 Oberon System 3 在 Raspberry Pi 3 上原生运行，并提供现成的 SD 卡镜像。此举显示了简约、单任务操作系统在现代单板计算机上的教育与试验潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/02aefac4-ea62-48db-9326-c0da373b11b8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran demands Bitcoin fees for ships passing Hormuz during ceasefire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗在停火期间要求通过霍尔木兹海峡的船只以比特币支付通行费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47690434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47690434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iran reportedly demands payments in Bitcoin for ships transiting Hormuz during a ceasefire, highlighting how states may experiment with crypto-enabled sanctions management. The move would complicate sanctions enforcement and reveal crypto as a potential tool for circumventing traditional financial channels. It raises questions about how policymakers monitor and regulate crypto in high-stakes geopolitical contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，伊朗在停火期间要求通过霍尔木兹海峡的船只以比特币支付通行费，显示出国家层面在制裁合规中的新探索。若属实，这将增加对传统金融体系的依赖被绕开的风險，也引发对加密货币跨境支付与监管的关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thealgorithmicbridge.com/p/ai-will-be-met-with-violence-and' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Will Be Met with Violence, and Nothing Good Will Come of It</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 将遭遇暴力对待，且结果不会好</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 274</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion argues that attempts to regulate or deploy AI will provoke violence or backlash, warning against simplistic technocratic approaches. It calls for thoughtful governance, ethical considerations, and inclusive design to avert harm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为对 AI 的监管与部署可能招致暴力反应和社会抵抗，警惕单一技术治理路径。呼吁在治理、伦理与包容性设计层面综合施策以避免负面后果。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rumproarious.com/2026/04/04/your-file-system-is-already-a-graph-database/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your File System Is Already A Graph Database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的文件系统其实已经是一个图数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that a filesystem naturally forms a graph, with folders, files, symlinks, and metadata creating nodes and edges. Viewing FS through a graph lens suggests new data-querying approaches directly on storage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出文件系统本质上就是一个图，目录、文件、符号链接与元数据共同构成节点和边。用图数据库思维去查询文件系统，可能带来对存储中关系的新洞察。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author surveys how to quantify code complexity and why it matters for maintainability and bugs. It covers metrics like cyclomatic and cognitive complexity, and offers pragmatic steps to simplify APIs, modularize code, and improve readability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论如何量化代码复杂度及其对维护性和缺陷风险的重要性。介绍常用度量如圈复杂度和认知复杂度，并给出简化接口、模块化和提升可读性等切实可行的改进建议。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 管理型代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47693047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47693047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude introduces Managed Agents, a feature that lets the AI orchestrate multiple tools and tasks autonomously with safety guardrails. This enables more complex, end-to-end workflows without constant human nudges, raising benefits for automation but also challenges around reliability, safety, and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 推出管理型代理功能，允许 AI 在受控条件下自动编排多种工具与任务。该特性可实现更复杂的端到端自动化，提升生产力，同时也带来可靠性、安全性与治理方面的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47702890' data-tags='Science,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.passiveradar.com/how-passive-radar-works/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Passive Radar Works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>被动雷达的工作原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47702890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47702890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how passive radar uses existing transmissions as illumination sources to detect objects without its own transmitter. By analyzing timing and Doppler shifts in reflections, passive radar enables stealthy or civilian surveillance but trades off resolution and clutter rejection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍被动雷达如何利用现有的传输信号作为照射源，在不自建发射的情况下探测目标。通过分析反射的到达时间与多普勒变化，被动雷达具有隐蔽性与较低成本的优势，但在分辨率与对杂波的处理上存在权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.passiveradar.com/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/Silentium_Defence_-_Passive_Radar_for_Space_Domain_Awareness_-_HIgher_Resolution_3-2-1-1.jpg' data-full='https://www.passiveradar.com/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/Silentium_Defence_-_Passive_Radar_for_Space_Domain_Awareness_-_HIgher_Resolution_3-2-1-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47709130' data-tags='Security,Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/aloshdenny/reverse-SynthID' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse engineering Gemini&#x27;s SynthID detection</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向工程 Gemini 的 SynthID 检测</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47709130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47709130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reverse engineering Gemini&#x27;s SynthID detection reveals what the system checks to verify authenticity, highlighting the fragility and design trade-offs of hardware-backed identity. The write-up underscores challenges in reliably detecting synthetic IDs and what this implies for future security tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Gemini 的 SynthID 检测进行逆向工程，揭示系统为验证真实身份而检查的要点，凸显硬件背书身份的脆弱性与设计权衡。该工作强调在可靠检测合成身份方面的挑战，以及对未来安全工具的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c16b48db0a8ab4b39e7e416d8cf46cc3897fe07fcf517e65aac9d3a219a20331/aloshdenny/reverse-SynthID' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c16b48db0a8ab4b39e7e416d8cf46cc3897fe07fcf517e65aac9d3a219a20331/aloshdenny/reverse-SynthID' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eualternative.eu/guides/building-saas-eu-stack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a SaaS in 2026 Using Only EU Infrastructure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 2026 年仅使用欧盟基础设施构建 SaaS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The guide argues you can build a compliant, data-sovereign SaaS using EU-hosted services and cloud providers, focusing on data residency, privacy, and regulatory alignment. It discusses tradeoffs, vendor lock-in, and building with European tooling to avoid cross-border risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章建议在 2026 年构建 SaaS 时，完全使用欧盟基础设施以确保数据主权与合规性，并讨论潜在的权衡与风险。内容覆盖数据驻留、隐私保护、供应商锁定以及以欧盟工具链为核心的架构设计。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47681274' data-tags='Finance,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nehanarula.org/2026/04/03/bitcoin-and-quantum-computing.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bitcoin and quantum computing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比特币与量子计算</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47681274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47681274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quantum computing poses a long-term risk to Bitcoin&#x27;s cryptographic foundations, since enough qubits could break current elliptic-curve signatures. The piece reviews the timeline uncertainties and the industry&#x27;s push toward post-quantum or quantum-resistant schemes, plus how exchanges and wallets should plan upgrades. While not imminent, the topic underscores the need for proactive crypto hygiene and upgrade paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>量子计算在长期内可能威胁比特币的椭圆曲线签名等加密基础。文章回顾时间线的不确定性以及行业推动的后量子防护方案，如交易所与钱包的升级计划。尽管威胁未在眼前，但提醒用户关注安全升级和密钥规程变更的前瞻性准备。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/solod-dev/solod' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solod – A subset of Go that translates to C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Solod：把 Go 的子集转译成 C 的工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47669337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47669337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solod is a subset of Go that compiles down to C, enabling tight interop and potential performance benefits without a full Go toolchain. The project aims to let developers write familiar Go-like code and target C for ecosystems where C is preferred or required.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Solod 是一种 Go 的子集，能够编译成 C，从而在无需完整 Go 工具链的情况下实现高效的互操作性。该项目让开发者用类似 Go 的语法编写代码，并以 C 作为目标，适用于需要或偏好 C 的场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1176200616/66f04c31-2452-4322-95be-55c5ac453ae4' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1176200616/66f04c31-2452-4322-95be-55c5ac453ae4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Polymarket accounts scored big on a precisely timed Iran ceasefire bet, underscoring how prediction markets can deliver rapid windfalls for timely bets. The story highlights liquidity, risk, and regulatory scrutiny in prediction markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新创建的 Polymarket 帐户在伊朗停火预测中的时机下注获得了不小的收益，凸显预测市场对及时下注的快速回报潜力。报道也揭示了这类市场的流动性、风险及监管审查的议题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/05/copilot-is-for-entertainment-purposes-only-according-to-microsofts-terms-of-service/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Copilot is &#x27;for entertainment purposes only&#x27;, per Microsoft&#x27;s terms of use</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft&#x27;s Copilot terms explicitly restrict use to entertainment purposes. This phrasing raises questions about production reliability and encourages guardrails and human review for code and content generated by AI.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oseifert.ch/blog/linux-kernel-pgit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I imported the full Linux kernel git history into pgit</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Migrating the Linux kernel&#x27;s entire Git history into pgit showcases how alternative Git tooling can speed up queries on large histories. It demonstrates practical gains in historical exploration, blame, and diffs for a massive project.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 Linux 内核的整个 Git 历史导入到 pgit，展示了替代型 Git 工具在大规模历史记录上提升查询效率的潜力。这个移植让对历史、 blame 与差异的探索在如此庞大项目中变得更高效。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://willybrauner.com/journal/signal-the-push-pull-based-algorithm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Signals, the push-pull based algorithm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Signals：一种基于推拉的算法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47637116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47637116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Signals introduces a push-pull based algorithm aimed at streamlining data flow by coordinating producers and consumers with lightweight signals. The approach can reduce latency and contention in reactive or streaming systems. It offers a mental model and potential performance improvements for systems design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Signals 介绍了一种基于推拉的算法，通过轻量级信号协调生产者和消费者来优化数据流，旨在降低反应式或流处理系统中的延迟和竞争。该方法提供了一个直观的设计模型，以及可能的性能改进方向。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://europe.mistral.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European AI. A playbook to own it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲 AI：掌控它的行动指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe&#x27;s playbook outlines strategies for building sovereign, ethical, and competitive AI ecosystems, balancing regulation with innovation. It emphasizes data governance, responsible AI, and regional leadership to reduce dependence on non-European tech stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份行动指南提出在监管与创新之间取得平衡，打造主权、道德且具竞争力的欧洲 AI 生态系统。文中强调数据治理、负责任的 AI，以及提升区域领导力，以减少对非欧洲技术体系的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://europe.mistral.ai/images/og.png' data-full='https://europe.mistral.ai/images/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47721955' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.nishantsoni.com/p/ive-seen-a-thousand-openclaw-deploys' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenClaw’s memory is unreliable, and you don’t know when it will break</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenClaw 的内存不可靠，何时会崩溃你也说不准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47721955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47721955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Real-world deployment reports show OpenClaw memory is unreliable, with failures that are unpredictable and hard to diagnose. The posts emphasize the fragility of some toolchains and warn operators to expect intermittent breaks that can ripple through systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多次在真实环境中的 OpenClaw 部署暴露出内存的不可靠性，故障不可预测且难以诊断。此现象凸显某些工具链的脆弱性，提醒运维要对间歇性中断保持警觉并做好鲁棒性准备。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!QhRF!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fnishantsoni.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1895258967%26version%3D9' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!QhRF!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fnishantsoni.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1895258967%26version%3D9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/randerson112/craft' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I built a Cargo-like build tool for C/C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我打造了一个类似 Cargo 的 C/C++ 构建工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47705413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47705413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Like Cargo for Rust, this tool aims to manage dependencies, build pipelines, and reproducible builds for C/C++. It could streamline large C/C++ projects, improve determinism, caching, and cross-language interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此工具模仿 Rust 的 Cargo，尝试为 C/C++ 项目管理依赖、构建流水线与可重复构建。它可能简化大规模项目的依赖与缓存管理，并提升跨语言生态的互操作性与可重复性。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47666488' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://photon.codes/blog/we-found-a-ticking-time-bomb-in-macos-tcp-networking' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A macOS bug that causes TCP networking to stop working after 49.7 days</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 的一个 bug：TCP 在约 49.7 天后网络停止工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47666488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47666488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A macOS networking bug causes TCP connections to fail after about 49.7 days of uptime, affecting long-running services and VPNs. The issue highlights the fragility of long-duration network stacks and could spur patching, with implications for stability in servers and enterprise deployments. Workarounds or kernel updates are likely in future releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 macOS 网络问题在大约 49.7 天运行后会导致 TCP 连接失败，影响长期运行的服务与 VPN。这暴露了长时运行网络栈的脆弱性，可能推动补丁与更新以提升系统稳定性，尤其对服务器和企业部署影响显著。未来版本可能提供修复或临时解决方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/YXr1OxGxAmSLi60593nzjskSwM.png?width=2400&amp;height=1260' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/YXr1OxGxAmSLi60593nzjskSwM.png?width=2400&amp;height=1260' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://friendi.ca/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Friendica – A Decentralized Social Network</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Friendica —— 一个去中心化的社交网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47648048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47648048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Friendica is a decentralized social network focused on privacy and federation across multiple protocols. It offers a community-driven alternative to centralized platforms, with interop options that appeal to users wary of data silos. The project emphasizes openness, moderation controls, and interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Friendica 是一个去中心化的社交网络，强调隐私与跨协议的联邦互通。它为用户提供相对于集中的平台的社区驱动型替代方案，支持多协议的互操作性。该项目强调开放性、可控的内容监管以及跨平台互联性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/business/2026/apr/11/polymarket-gamblers-betting-iran-war-ukraine-news-truth' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Polymarket gamblers betting millions on war</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Polymarket 赌客在战争相关事件上下注数百万</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47729994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47729994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Polymarket users are placing multi-million-dollar bets on war-related outcomes, drawing scrutiny from regulators and highlighting the financial appetite for real-world event prediction markets. The activity underscores both the potential of decentralized markets to price geopolitical risk and the regulatory and ethical questions they raise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Polymarket 用户在战争相关事件上下注数百万美元，引发监管关注并暴露了对现实世界事件预测市场的资金热潮。此现象既显示了去中心化市场定价地缘政治风险的潜力，也凸显监管与伦理方面的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ucdavis.edu/health/news/penguin-toxicologists-find-pfas-chemicals-remote-patagonia' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Penguin &#x27;Toxicologists&#x27; Find PFAS Chemicals in Remote Patagonia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>企鹅‘毒理学家’在偏远巴塔哥尼亚发现 PFAS 化学物质</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47714273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47714273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wildlife toxicology teams found PFAS contaminants in penguins in remote Patagonia, indicating widespread environmental contamination. The finding highlights biomonitoring value and potential ecological and human health implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>偏远的巴塔哥尼亚地区企鹅体内发现 PFAS 材料，显示环境污染广泛存在。该发现凸显生物监测的价值，以及对生态与人类健康的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores implementing persistent data structures in a Clojure-on-Fennel setup, showing how to reason about immutability, sharing, and performance in a Lua-hosted Lisp environment. <p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tech.stonecharioteer.com/posts/2026/traceroute/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding Traceroute</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>理解 traceroute 的工作原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical primer on how traceroute works: TTL-based probing, ICMP responses, and interpreting hop data. It also notes limitations like firewall filtering and path asymmetry that can mislead troubleshooting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 traceroute 的工作机制：通过基于生存时间的探测、ICMP 响应以及对跳点数据的解读。也指出防火墙、路径不对称等局限，可能使故障排查出现偏差。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tech.stonecharioteer.com/images/posts/traceroute/concept.png' data-full='https://tech.stonecharioteer.com/images/posts/traceroute/concept.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(243) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Netflix/void-model' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VOID: Video Object and Interaction Deletion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VOID：视频对象与互动删除</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47627998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47627998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netflix&#x27;s VOID model enables targeted deletion of objects and interactions within videos, supporting privacy-preserving edits and safer content handling. By focusing on object-level removal, it aims to reduce re-encoding overhead and preserve remaining content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Netflix 的 VOID 模型实现对视频中的特定对象和互动进行逐位删除，支持隐私保护的内容编辑与更安全的内容处理。通过面向对象级别的删除，旨在降低重新编码的开销，同时尽量保留其余部分。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7a209b6e6387732703ee12b7b82de779b8b7ff3bb728a657e0efde85ce50a382/Netflix/void-model' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7a209b6e6387732703ee12b7b82de779b8b7ff3bb728a657e0efde85ce50a382/Netflix/void-model' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高效拖延术</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Productive Procrastination argues that deliberate, structured delay can yield real progress by pairing low-friction tasks with bigger goals. It offers techniques like structured procrastination, timeboxing, and prioritization to turn distraction into momentum.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《高效拖延术》提出通过刻意、结构化的拖延来推动实际产出，将低门槛任务与更大目标绑定。文中介绍结构化拖延、时间盒管理和优先级策略等方法，将分心转化为行动的动力。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FluidCAD brings parametric CAD to the web with JavaScript, enabling models defined as code and driven by scripts. This approach enables rapid iteration, versioning, and integration with web tooling, signaling a shift toward programmable design tools for engineers and hobbyists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FluidCAD 将参数化 CAD 带到网页上，模型以代码形式定义并可通过脚本驱动。此设计将支持快速迭代、版本管理，并更好地与网页工具链集成，体现了工程与爱好者在设计工具上的可编程化趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers spend more time running and testing code than reading it, according to recent reflections. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为开发者花在运行与测试代码上的时间多于阅读代码。文章强调应优先考虑执行、测试和可观测行为的工具链与工作流，影响教育、调试与持续集成/交付等方面。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.farzon.org/2026/04/binary-obfuscation-that-doesnt-kill-lto.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Binary obfuscation that doesn&#x27;t kill LTO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不会破坏 LTO 的二进制混淆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47653642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47653642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a binary obfuscation approach that preserves Link Time Optimization, showing how security can be bolstered without sacrificing performance or compile-time benefits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一种在不损害链接时优化（LTO）的前提下实现的二进制混淆方法，展示如何在不牺牲性能与编译时优化的情况下提升安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/a/AVvXsEhPJQN6qeVy8lr8Rikfss5p82ORiNryYFRobl5Xunt61-vavRJxGqxlZeMWtzNsWV8MYWvIh6Ux4v-15tzmb-PX9oote0ptZ5d31jYyEkuGguWGEqQpYsJpv20IFnSL7GLflaO8jiXux8v13zhAskwEyNRXE9jOYmg1XScBXWe_9pG1LqlSVjPAlBWrdKc=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/a/AVvXsEhPJQN6qeVy8lr8Rikfss5p82ORiNryYFRobl5Xunt61-vavRJxGqxlZeMWtzNsWV8MYWvIh6Ux4v-15tzmb-PX9oote0ptZ5d31jYyEkuGguWGEqQpYsJpv20IFnSL7GLflaO8jiXux8v13zhAskwEyNRXE9jOYmg1XScBXWe_9pG1LqlSVjPAlBWrdKc=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
