<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47705324' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://iucn.org/press-release/202604/emperor-penguin-and-antarctic-fur-seal-now-endangered-due-climate-change-iucn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emperor penguin and Antarctic fur seal now endangered</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帝企鹅和南极毛皮海豹现已濒危</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47705324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47705324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Climate change and warming oceans have pushed emperor penguins and Antarctic fur seals to endangered status per the IUCN. The designation underscores heightened risks to Antarctic ecosystems and highlights the urgency of conservation and climate mitigation efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>气候变化与海洋变暖将帝企鹅和南极毛皮海豹推向濒危， IUCN 的最新评估显示这两者均处于高风险。此情形凸显南极生态系统所面临的日益严峻的威胁，促使加强保护与气候缓解措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://iucn.org/sites/default/files/2026-04/emperor-penguin-chicks-on-rothschild-island_photo.jpg' data-full='https://iucn.org/sites/default/files/2026-04/emperor-penguin-chicks-on-rothschild-island_photo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47694983' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://swift.org/blog/expanding-swift-ide-support/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Expanding Swift&#x27;s IDE Support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扩展 Swift 的 IDE 支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47694983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47694983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Swift.org is broadening tooling support across IDEs to improve autocompletion, diagnostics, and integration beyond Xcode. This move lowers barriers for Swift adoption in diverse editor workflows and strengthens developer experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Swift.org 正在扩大对 IDE 的工具支持，提升自动完成、诊断与跨编辑器集成的体验，帮助开发者在多编辑器工作流中更高效使用 Swift。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://swift.org/apple-touch-icon-180x180.png' data-full='https://swift.org/apple-touch-icon-180x180.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47634422' data-tags='Science,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/science/science-news/native-americans-dice-games-probability-study-rcna266426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Native Americans had dice 12k years ago</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美洲原住民1.2万年前已有骰子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47634422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47634422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists report 12,000-year-old dice from North America, indicating that dice gaming and probabilistic thinking have deep roots on the continent. The find adds nuance to our view of prehistoric leisure and mathematics, suggesting complex social play was present far earlier than previously thought.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家发现北美地区早于一万两千年的骰子，表明该地区早在史前就有掷骰游戏和概率思维。此发现为人们对原始娱乐和数学思维的理解增添新细节，显示在此前人类社会就已进行复杂的共同游戏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-04/260402-Early-examples-of-Native-American-dice-ew-132p-fbf33e.jpg' data-full='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-04/260402-Early-examples-of-Native-American-dice-ew-132p-fbf33e.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47703065' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://getsession.org/donate' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Session is shutting down in 90 days</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Session 将在90天内关闭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47703065' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47703065' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Session will shut down in 90 days, highlighting the funding and maintenance challenges facing privacy-focused tools. The closure serves as a reminder that sustainable support is essential for open-source projects with niche value.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Session 将在 90 天后关闭，凸显隐私导向工具在长期资金与维护方面的挑战。该决定提醒人们，对特定价值的开源项目需要可持续的支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://getsession.org/assets/images/session-hero.png' data-full='https://getsession.org/assets/images/session-hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.danvk.org/2026/03/08/oldnyc-updates.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI helps add 10k more photos to OldNYC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 让 OldNYC 增添一万张照片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI augmentation adds about 10,000 new photos to OldNYC, expanding the historical record of New York through computer vision and data curation. The update enhances accessibility for researchers and hobbyists exploring urban change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 AI 增强，OldNYC 增添约1万张新照片，扩充纽约市历史记录。此更新提升了研究者与爱好者在研究城市变迁时的可访问性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.danvk.org/images/732679f-a.jpg' data-full='https://www.danvk.org/images/732679f-a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/linebender/xilem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xilem – An experimental Rust native UI framework</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xilem：一个实验性的 Rust 原生 UI 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47682719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47682719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xilem is an experimental Rust-native UI framework targeting high performance and clean abstractions with native widgets and minimal runtime overhead. It appeals to developers seeking efficient, low-level UI tech in Rust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xilem 是一个实验性的 Rust 原生 UI 框架，致力于提供高性能、简洁的抽象和原生控件。项目强调最小运行时开销和跨平台潜力，吸引对本地界面开发感兴趣的开发者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e55abec780f7abe4fac0ac734a4fff0a516ab4e013935b1a1d5bf13ab311d130/linebender/xilem' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e55abec780f7abe4fac0ac734a4fff0a516ab4e013935b1a1d5bf13ab311d130/linebender/xilem' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://taintedcoders.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bevy game development tutorials and in-depth resources</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bevy 游戏开发教程与深入资源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47698111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47698111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bevy tutorials and resources provide a structured entry into Rust-based game development, covering ECS architecture, rendering, and tooling. The collection helps both beginners and seasoned developers level up with practical guides and deep-dive materials.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bevy 的游戏开发教程与深度资料为 Rust 游戏开发者提供系统化学习路径，涵盖 ECS 架构、渲染和工具链等要点，适合初学者与有经验者深入学习。</p>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47656981' data-tags='Business,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://drop.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Drop, formerly Massdrop, ends most collaborations and rebrands under Corsair</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Drop：前 Massdrop 终止大部分合作并以 Corsair 名义重塑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47656981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Drop, formerly Massdrop, is ending many collaborations and rebranding under Corsair. The move signals a strategic consolidation around core gaming peripherals and community-driven products, affecting creators and fans who followed limited releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Drop（前身 Massdrop）终止多数合作并以 Corsair 名义重新定位。此举标志着向核心游戏外设与社区驱动产品的策略整合，对曾关注限量商品的创作者与粉丝将产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.corsair.com/image/upload/akamai/img/reusable/corsair-logo-white.svg' data-full='https://assets.corsair.com/image/upload/akamai/img/reusable/corsair-logo-white.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47661065' data-tags='Politics,Culture,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/culture/infinite-scroll/the-team-behind-a-pro-iran-lego-themed-viral-video-campaign' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The team behind a pro-Iran, Lego-themed viral-video campaign</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>背后团队推动的亲伊朗、乐高风格的病毒式视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47661065' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47661065' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pro-Iran Lego-themed viral video campaign reveals how lightweight, playful content can go viral in geopolitics, bypassing gatekeepers and shaping perceptions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一支亲伊朗、乐高风格的病毒式视频背后，展示了轻量、趣味内容在地缘政治环境中的快速传播，以及对舆论的影响力。报道揭示了虚假信息、平台治理以及数字劝导工具箱正在演化的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/69cd2632d1995bc37f7a4516/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/NewYorker_brainrotdiplomacy_final_2.jpg' data-full='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/69cd2632d1995bc37f7a4516/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/NewYorker_brainrotdiplomacy_final_2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47718830' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/pcmasterrace/comments/1sh4e5l/warning_hwmonitor_163_download_on_the_official/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CPU-Z and HWMonitor compromised</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CPU-Z 与 HWMonitor 被入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47718830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47718830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two popular hardware monitoring tools were compromised, likely via bundled installers or supply-chain attack, risking user systems. The incident underscores the importance of provenance checks, software signing, and consumer caution when downloading tools that access low-level hardware data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两款流行的硬件监控工具遭到入侵，可能通过捆绑安装包或供应链攻击实现，威胁到用户系统安全。此事件强调追踪来源、软件签名和下载此类访问硬件底层信息工具的谨慎性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47707762' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hegel.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hegel, a universal property-based testing protocol and family of PBT libraries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hegel：一种通用属性测试协议及其 PBT 库家族</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47707762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47707762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hegel proposes a universal protocol for property-based testing to standardize how tests describe properties and shrink results across languages, enabling reusable test strategies. The ecosystem includes a family of libraries implementing the protocol, improving cross-language testing and test-case generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hegel 提出一种通用的属性基测试协议，旨在跨语言标准化如何描述属性并收敛测试用例。相关生态包括若干实现该协议的 PBT 库，提升跨语言测试的一致性与用例生成效率。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tech valuations have fallen back to pre-AI-boom levels, signaling a normalization of investor sentiment after AI hype. The reset may separate hype from fundamentals, presenting risks and opportunities for investors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科技公司的估值已回落至AI繁荣前的水平，显示在AI 热潮之后投资者情绪回归理性。估值的回落可能将热度与基本面区分开来，给投资者带来潜在的机会与风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/2026/04/06/tofolli-gates/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tofolli gates are all you need</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Toffoli 门才是你需要的全部</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Toffoli gates show that a single gate family is enough to implement any computation when combined with negation, illustrating the elegance of universal gate sets in reversible and quantum circuits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张，在只用 Toffoli 门且可与简单取反结合的前提下，任何计算都可以实现，揭示了可逆与量子电路中统一门集的强大魅力。它还讨论了在硬件实现和教学中的实际意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2022/05/twittercard.png' data-full='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2022/05/twittercard.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dr. Dobb&#x27;s Developer Library DVD 6 compiles vintage programming resources, including code samples, tutorials, and tools from a bygone era. It serves as a historical snapshot of how developers learned and shared knowledge before the web era, offering both nostalgia and practical insights for retro computing fans. The collection can inspire modern learners to appreciate enduring programming patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dr. Dobb&#x27;s 开发者图书馆 DVD 6 汇集了一批老派的编程资源，包括代码示例、教程与工具，代表一个已逝时代的学习方式。它是开发者如何在网络兴起前获取知识的历史写照，既有怀旧价值，也提供对复古计算的实用见解。该合集可能激发现代开发者对常用编程模式的重新认识。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kerkour.com/rust-supply-chain-nightmare' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Supply chain nightmare: How Rust will be attacked and what we can do to mitigate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 供应链噩梦：将如何被攻击及应对办法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47718891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47718891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of Rust&#x27;s supply chain risks, including crates.io vulnerabilities, dependency confusion, and malicious crates. The piece outlines mitigation strategies: strict lockfiles, reproducible builds, code signing, and vendor-backed crates. It emphasizes need for ecosystem-wide security practices as Rust grows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章探讨 Rust 生态的供应链风险，包括 crates.io 漏洞、依赖混淆和恶意 crate。提出的缓释策略包括使用严格的锁定文件、可重复构建、代码签名，以及对关键 crate 的自有代理，强调在 Rust 生态日益壮大的背景下需要全面的安全实践。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All elementary functions from a single binary operator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用单一二元运算生成所有初等函数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47746610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47746610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus. The result has implications for symbolic computation, functional programming, and universal function representations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。这一发现可能影响符号计算、函数式编程以及统一表示函数的方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ubuntu.com/blog/risc-v-101-what-is-it-and-what-does-it-mean-for-canonical' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is RISC-V and why it matters to Canonical</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47691538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 119</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47691538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canonical&#x27;s Ubuntu ecosystem is examining RISC-V, the open instruction set architecture that promotes customization and openness. The move signals growing interest in open hardware for cloud, edge, and embedded workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canonical 的 Ubuntu 生态正在探索 RISC-V 开放指令集，强调开放硬件的潜力与定制能力。此举体现业界在云、边缘和嵌入式场景对可控架构的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.intelligentliving.co/scientists-finally-figured-out-how-eels-reproduce/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists Figured Out How Eels Reproduce (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家终于揭示鳗鱼繁殖方式（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47607408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 119</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47607408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists reveal how eels reproduce, solving a long-standing biological mystery. The findings describe spawning in the wild lifecycle and lifecycle steps, contributing to fisheries management and evolutionary biology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家揭示了鳗鱼的繁殖方式，解决长期的生物学之谜，阐明了它们的繁殖与迁徙模式。研究表明鳗鱼在深海产卵的生命周期，相关发现有助于保护、养殖与生物学研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.intelligentliving.co/wp-content/uploads/2022/11/Scientists-Track-Eels-to-Their-Ocean-Breeding-Grounds-in-World2.jpg' data-full='https://www.intelligentliving.co/wp-content/uploads/2022/11/Scientists-Track-Eels-to-Their-Ocean-Breeding-Grounds-in-World2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A teardown of an unreleased LG rollable phone reveals mechanical complexity, reliability concerns, and cost drivers that hinder mainstream adoption. The insights explain why rollables haven&#x27;t yet taken off in the market.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kitfunso/hippo-memory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Hippo, biologically inspired memory for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Hippo——生物启发的AI代理记忆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47667672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47667672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hippo proposes memory structures for AI agents inspired by biological systems, aiming to improve long-horizon reasoning and continual learning. By borrowing from how real brains store and retrieve information, it may offer more scalable and robust agent cognition. If you&#x27;re building persistent, capable agents, this approach is worth tracking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hippo 提出受生物系统启发的 AI 代理记忆结构，目标是提升长期推理和持续学习的能力。借鉴真实大脑的信息存储与检索机制，或许能带来更可扩展、更稳健的代理认知。若在构建具持久能力的智能代理，值得关注。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An accessible, entertaining explanation of the Raft consensus algorithm using clips from Mean Girls; it clarifies concepts like leader election, consensus safety, and liveness with tangible analogies. The piece helps engineers grasp how Raft ensures reliability in distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过《Mean Girls》中的情节，为 Raft 共识算法提供直观解释，用可感的比喻解释领导者选举、共识安全与活性等要点。帮助读者理解 Raft 如何在分布式系统中保障可靠性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study on recovering a corrupted 12 TB multi-device pool details salvage steps, including data recovery, metadata repair, and minimizing further damage. It underscores the importance of backups and careful recovery procedures for multi-disk arrays. Practical guidance for disaster recovery and data integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>案例研究记录了从损坏的 12 TB 多设备池中恢复数据的过程，涵盖数据挽救、元数据修复及降低进一步损坏的做法。强调了备份与谨慎恢复在多盘阵列中的重要性，为灾难恢复与数据完整性提供实用要点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://has-electricity-decoupled-yet.strommarktberatung.de' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Has electricity decoupled from natural gas prices in Germany?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国电价是否已脱离天然气价格？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47675372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47675372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It investigates whether German electricity prices have decoupled from natural gas prices, with results suggesting a growing separation driven by renewables and structural market reforms. The finding has implications for consumer bills, energy policy, and hedging strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析显示，德国电价可能正逐步与天然气价格脱钩，这与可再生能源扩大、市场改革等因素有关。此现象对家庭电费、能源政策及对冲策略具有重要影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eli.thegreenplace.net/2026/watgo-a-webassembly-toolkit-for-go/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Watgo – A WebAssembly Toolkit for Go</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Watgo：Go 的 WebAssembly 工具链</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47722262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47722262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Watgo provides a WebAssembly toolkit for Go, enabling Go programs to compile to Wasm and interoperate with JavaScript or host environments. The project illustrates the growing use of WASM for cross-language portability and edge computing, making Go usable in browser-like or sandboxed runtimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Watgo 为 Go 提供 WebAssembly 工具链，使 Go 程序能够编译为 Wasm，并在与 JavaScript/宿主环境的协作中运行。该项目体现了 WASM 在跨语言移植和边缘计算场景的日益重要性，让 Go 在浏览器或沙箱运行时具备更广的应用。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47613827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47613827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigative piece reveals how a single ant can fetch hundreds of dollars, signaling a growing niche in wildlife trafficking. It discusses supply chains, enforcement challenges, and biodiversity risks as demand for rare insects rises. The takeaway is a warning about how niche trades complicate protection efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇调查揭示，一只蚂蚁竟能卖到约 220 美元，显示野生动物走私正在出现新的细分市场。文中探讨了供给链、执法挑战以及对生物多样性的威胁，随着对稀有昆虫的需求增加，执法难度也在上升。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eetimes.com/taking-on-cuda-with-rocm-one-step-after-another/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Taking on CUDA with ROCm: &#x27;One Step After Another&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以 ROCm 逐步挑战 CUDA</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47745284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47745284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD&#x27;s ROCm roadmap aims to compete with CUDA through incremental improvements, focusing on portability, kernel compilation, and performance optimizations. The approach underscores the difficulty of breaking NVIDIA&#x27;s software moat and the need for strong open standards in GPU computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 的 ROCm 计划通过渐进改进与 CUDA 竞争，聚焦可移植性、内核编译与性能优化。该策略凸显打破 NVIDIA 软件生态护城河的难度，同时也强调在 GPU 计算领域坚持开源标准的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KL 散度的六个半直觉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47678690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47678690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents six core intuitions about KL divergence (plus a half), clarifying how it measures distribution mismatch, asymmetry, and data-efficiency in learning. The notes help practitioners reason about when KL-based objectives help or hurt model training.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>给出关于 KL 散度的六个核心直觉（再加半个），阐明其如何衡量分布差异、非对称性和学习中的数据效率。笔记帮助从业者判断在模型训练中何时使用 KL 目标有益、何时可能带来负面影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://39669.cdn.cke-cs.com/rQvD3VnunXZu34m86e5f/images/8e445e60018b63fa5aa882b18a5f693bce4776cf2a707f95.png' data-full='https://39669.cdn.cke-cs.com/rQvD3VnunXZu34m86e5f/images/8e445e60018b63fa5aa882b18a5f693bce4776cf2a707f95.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lalitm.com/til-number-in-man-page-titles-e-g-sleep-3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Number in man page titles e.g. sleep(3)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>手册页标题中的数字，例如 sleep(3)</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47658743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47658743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Why do man pages include the section number? Sleep(3) is not a version but a section in the Unix manual; the post explains practical tips for navigating and citing manual entries. It’s a concise reminder for developers and sysadmins to be precise when consulting docs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为何手册页要标注数字？sleep(3) 不是版本号，而是 Unix 手册中的一个章节。文中给出在查阅和引用文档时的实用技巧，提醒开发者和系统管理员要保持引用的准确性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47676174' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cis.upenn.edu/~bcpierce/papers/mysteriesofdropbox.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mysteries of Dropbox: Testing of a Distributed Sync Service (2016) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dropbox 的谜团：分布式同步服务的属性测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Property-based testing is applied to scale-tested distributed sync in Dropbox, probing invariants under network delays and partial failures. The work highlights how such techniques expose edge-case bugs that traditional tests miss and suggests practical testing strategies for complex distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将属性测试应用到 Dropbox 的分布式同步系统，检验在网络延迟和部分故障下的系统不变量。此研究揭示传统测试容易漏检的边缘情况，并提出提升分布式系统健壮性的实用测试方法。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47636579' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/roscopeco/anos' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Anos – a hand-written ~100KiB microkernel for x86-64 and RISC-V</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Anos——一个手写的 ~100KiB 的微内核，支持 x86-64 与 RISC-V</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47636579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47636579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anos is a hand-written microkernel around 100 KiB supporting x86-64 and RISC-V, designed for exploration of OS fundamentals in tiny footprints. It serves as a learning and research vehicle for kernel design and cross-architecture ideas. It’s a niche project, but interesting for students and OS enthusiasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anos 是一个约 100 KiB 的手写微内核，支持 x86-64 与 RISC-V，用于探索极小体量系统中的操作系统基础。它可以作为内核设计与跨架构思路的学习与研究载体，对计算机系学生和操作系统爱好者具有吸引力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.demandsphere.com/blog/rebuilding-demandsphere-with-jekyll-and-claude-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moving from WordPress to Jekyll (and static site generators in general)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47710007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes rebuilding a site with Jekyll after WordPress, highlighting gains in performance, security, and simplicity. It also reflects on the broader shift to static site generators and how tooling like Claude can complement content workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述从 WordPress 迁移到 Jekyll 的过程，强调加载速度、安全性和维护简化等收益，并讨论静态站点生成器的趋势及相关工具在内容工作流中的互补作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.psypost.org/intelligent-people-are-better-judges-of-the-intelligence-of-others/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intelligent people are better judges of the intelligence of others</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>聪明的人更善于判断他人的智力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Psychology study finds that people with higher intelligence are better at judging others&#x27; intelligence, suggesting cognitive ability influences social perception and bias. The result informs how we interpret expert opinions and who is trusted in assessments of ability. It also prompts reflection on fairness and humility in judgment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项心理学研究发现，智力水平更高的人在判断他人智力方面更为准确，表明认知能力影响社会认知与偏见。这一发现有助于理解为何某些人更易被视为权威，以及在评估能力时应保持谦逊与公平。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47675155' data-tags='Programming'>

<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inngest.com/blog/hanging-promises-for-control-flow' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can&#x27;t cancel a JavaScript promise (except sometimes you can)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你不能取消 JavaScript 的 Promise（有时可以）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47675155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47675155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JavaScript promises cannot be cancelled natively, but patterns exist to emulate cancellation such as AbortController and cancellation tokens.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JavaScript 的 Promise 原生不能取消，但可以通过 AbortController 等模式实现“取消”效果。文中解析了实际应用中的坑点与可靠控制流的常用做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.inngest.com/assets/blog/hanging-promises-for-control-flow/featured-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.inngest.com/assets/blog/hanging-promises-for-control-flow/featured-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Restoration videos of the Apollo Guidance Computer showcase early spacecraft computing hardware, revealing how engineers wrestled with limited memory and real-time constraints. The clips highlight the ingenuity of vintage embedded systems and offer lessons for hardware preservation and emulation in modern projects.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Investigating How Long-Distance Couples Use Digital Games to Facilitate Intimacy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究远距离情侣如何通过电子游戏增进亲密感</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study documents how couples in long-distance relationships use digital games to maintain closeness, shaping interactions, intimacy, and relationship satisfaction. It offers takeaways for designers of social games and remote communication tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究记录了远距离情侣如何通过数字游戏维持情感亲密，影响互动模式、情感表达与关系满意度。研究结果对社交游戏设计和远程沟通工具的发展提供了可转化的洞察。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47662658' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/andmarti1424/sc-im' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sc-im: Spreadsheets in your terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sc-im：终端中的电子表格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sc-im is a terminal-based spreadsheet editor that brings spreadsheet capabilities to the command line, with keyboard-driven navigation. It appeals to power users who need quick data manipulation on headless or low-resource environments, without leaving the terminal. For lightweight workflows, it can be a surprisingly productive alternative to GUI spreadsheets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sc-im 是一个在终端中运行的电子表格编辑器，支持键盘驱动的操作，直接在命令行里完成数据处理。它特别适合在无头或资源受限的环境下进行快速数据操作的高阶用户，无需离开终端即可完成工作。对于轻量级的工作流，这是一个出人意料的高效替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f7b39576e25c316f8f6048a144660dbf0e28844753f8e2bea742f388a88ca1a0/andmarti1424/sc-im' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f7b39576e25c316f8f6048a144660dbf0e28844753f8e2bea742f388a88ca1a0/andmarti1424/sc-im' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47683376' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discourse.llvm.org/t/rfc-jsir-a-high-level-ir-for-javascript/90456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JSIR: A High-Level IR for JavaScript</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JSIR：面向 JavaScript 的高级中间表示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47683376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47683376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JSIR proposes a high-level intermediate representation for JavaScript to improve compiler optimizations and cross-tooling. It aims to bridge JS engines with LLVM-like backends, enabling more portable and aggressive optimizations while preserving JS semantics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JSIR 提出一种面向 JavaScript 的高级中间表示，旨在提升编译器优化与跨工具链的能力。它试图将 JS 引擎与类似 LLVM 的后端衔接，以实现更可移植、且更具侵袭性的优化，同时保留 JavaScript 的语义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://us1.discourse-cdn.com/flex021/uploads/llvm/original/3X/a/5/a5e2f18e2787b0f7493d9aa43cc2f4d488794c53.png' data-full='https://us1.discourse-cdn.com/flex021/uploads/llvm/original/3X/a/5/a5e2f18e2787b0f7493d9aa43cc2f4d488794c53.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47689793' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://solidean.com/blog/2026/how-much-linear-memory-access-is-enough/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How much linear memory access is enough?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>线性内存访问到底需要多少？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questions how much linear memory access a program truly needs, challenging the assumption that more bandwidth always helps. It advocates for data locality, cache-friendly layouts, and practical heuristics to avoid memory bottlenecks in modern software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨程序对线性内存访问的真实需求，质疑“带宽越多越好”的普遍观点。强调数据局部性、缓存友好布局与在现代软件中避免内存瓶颈的实用方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://solidean.com/images/og/2026/bench-linear-memory-access.jpg' data-full='https://solidean.com/images/og/2026/bench-linear-memory-access.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47738864' data-tags='AI,Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(344) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nerdy.dev/why-ai-sucks-at-front-end' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why AI Sucks at Front End</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 为什么在前端开发中表现劣势</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI struggles with frontend tasks due to nuanced UI/UX, accessibility, and component composition that require human judgment. The piece argues AI is best used as an assistant rather than a replacement for frontend engineers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 在前端开发中难以处理细粒度的用户体验、无障碍与组件组合等问题，易出错。文章认为应将 AI 作为辅助工具，而非试图替代有经验的前端工程师。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nerdy.dev/media/why-ai-sucks-at-front-end.jpg' data-full='https://nerdy.dev/media/why-ai-sucks-at-front-end.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(346) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twill.ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Twill.ai (YC S25) – Delegate to cloud agents, get back PRs</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47720418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47720418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Twill.ai bills itself as a platform that delegates tasks to cloud agents and returns pull requests, effectively outsourcing collaboration to AI-driven automation. For dev teams, it promises faster iteration, but raises questions about security, governance, and auditability of autonomous agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Twill.ai 宣称通过云代理执行任务并返回 PRs，将协作工作交给 AI 驱动的自动化系统。对开发团队而言意味着更快迭代，同时也引出安全、治理与可审计性的问题。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>埃姆多克勒斯在纸草卷上发现的未知诗句</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47697722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47697722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A newly discovered papyrus fragment contains verses attributed to the pre-Socratic philosopher Empedocles, offering fresh evidence of his poetry. The find enriches our understanding of ancient Greek thought and the transmission of his work, with possible revisions to readings of his elemental theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份新发现的纸草卷证据显示埃姆多克勒斯的未知诗句，为其诗作提供新的证据，丰富了对古希腊思想及其文本传承的理解，可能对其四元素理论的解读产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/articles/mechanical-sympathy-principles.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Principles of Mechanical Sympathy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机械同情原则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47675731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47675731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler&#x27;s concept of aligning software with the hardware it runs on is explored, arguing that teams should optimize for the entire stack rather than chasing fastest code in isolation. The piece outlines principles like reducing hidden costs, choosing appropriate abstractions, and designing for maintainability alongside performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解读 Martin Fowler 的机械同情理念，强调让软件设计与底层硬件协同工作，而不是只追求局部极致性能。文章提出降低隐藏成本、选择合适的抽象，以及在可维护性与性能之间取得平衡的设计要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinfowler.com/articles/mechanical-sympathy-principles/card.png' data-full='https://martinfowler.com/articles/mechanical-sympathy-principles/card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47673379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47673379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeiMOS is a MOS 6502 superoptimizer that searches for tiny, fast instruction sequences; a retrocomputing tool that pushes the boundaries of optimization on classic hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeiMOS 是一款面向 MOS 6502 的超级优化器，能够搜索更小更快的指令序列，推动经典硬件上的优化边界。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Preliminary findings claim that pairing spicy foods with mint dramatically increases anti-inflammatory effects, potentially by hundreds of times. Real-world replication is needed, but the result is intriguing for nutrition science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有研究称辣味食品与薄荷结合能显著提升抗炎效果，甚至达到百倍以上的增幅。需在现实情景中进一步验证，但为营养与药理研究提供了新的线索。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on &#x27;greytrapping&#x27; over eighteen years questions whether the quirky method&#x27;s payoff is finally showing as defenses improve. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对“Greytrapping”在过去18年的回顾提出质疑：在防御日益加强的背景下，这一怪异做法的“回报”是否终于显现。文章讨论在持续的实验中新奇性与可靠性之间的张力，以及评估非常规技术时耐心的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47685984' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipsandcheese.com/p/investigating-split-locks-on-x86' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Investigating Split Locks on x86-64</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 x86-64 上的分割锁调查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47685984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47685984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains what split locks are in x86-64, their impact on performance and correctness in concurrent code, and how modern CPUs and programming practices mitigate them. It provides practical guidance for developers to avoid split-lock pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章阐述了 x86-64 架构中的分割锁（split locks）是什么、它们对并发代码的性能与正确性的影响，以及现代 CPU 与编程实践如何降低风险。为开发者提供了避免分割锁陷阱的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent Reading Test is likely a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents&#x27; reading comprehension and in-text reasoning. It highlights how agents interpret instructions, extract facts, and answer questions, informing how we measure progress in natural language understanding for agents. It can help guide future dataset design and evaluation metrics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代理阅读测试是一套用于评估AI代理阅读理解与文本推理能力的基准。它关注代理如何解读指令、提取事实并回答问题，为衡量代理在自然语言理解方面的进展提供指标。对于数据集设计和评估标准具有参考价值。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年的缩放式 UI：Prezi、impress.js，以及我为何做出不同的方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47665194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47665194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece surveys zooming user interfaces like Prezi and impress.js and explains why the author chose a different approach. It reflects on how panning, zooming, and 3D navigation shape user attention and information hierarchy, and why a distinct UI paradigm may better suit modern storytelling or data exploration. Good read for product designers and frontend developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章回顾了 Prezi、impress.js 等缩放式 UI 的趋势，并解释作者为何选择不同的实现方式。内容聚焦平移、缩放和三维导航如何影响用户注意力与信息层级，以及为何一种更独特的 UI 模型可能更适用于现代叙事与数据探索。对产品设计与前端开发者很有启发。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ansto.gov.au/news/artemis-ii-and-invisible-hazard-on-way-to-moon-part-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II and the invisible hazard on the way to the Moon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿耳忒弥斯 II号任务：月球之路上的隐形风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47714573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47714573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Artemis II faces unseen hazards for crewed lunar missions, including radiation exposure, long-duration isolation, and system reliability under deep-space conditions. The piece argues for rigorous risk management and robust life-support and shielding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯 II 号任务面临对载人月球任务的隐形风险，如辐射暴露、长期孤立与深空条件下系统可靠性。强调需要严格的风险管理、强健的生命支持和防护措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ansto.gov.au/sites/default/files/styles/hero_image/public/hero-images/Earth%20and%20moon_0.jpg?h=498e3814&amp;itok=c7GdPYbY' data-full='https://www.ansto.gov.au/sites/default/files/styles/hero_image/public/hero-images/Earth%20and%20moon_0.jpg?h=498e3814&amp;itok=c7GdPYbY' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47733066' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://noirlab.edu/public/news/noirlab2611/?nocache=true&amp;lang=en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New synthesis of astronomical measurements shows Hubble tension is real</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新一轮天文观测综合显示哈勃张力确实存在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A synthesis of astronomical measurements reinforces the Hubble tension, showing a persistent discrepancy in the universe’s expansion rate across datasets. The result fuels discussions on potential new physics or unaccounted systematics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多项天文观测的综合结果进一步强化了哈勃张力的存在，显示在宇宙膨胀速率的测量中存在持续的不一致。其影响在于引发对新物理学或潜在系统误差的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://storage.noirlab.edu/media/archives/images/screen/noirlab2611a.jpg' data-full='http://storage.noirlab.edu/media/archives/images/screen/noirlab2611a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dropbox.tech/infrastructure/improving-storage-efficiency-in-magic-pocket-our-immutable-blob-store' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Improving storage efficiency in Magic Pocket, Dropbox&#x27;s immutable blob store</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在 Dropbox 的不可变 Blob 存储 Magic Pocket 中提升存储效率的做法，包括去重、压缩与编码优化，同时保持不可变性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/agberohq/keeper' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Keeper – embedded secret store for Go (help me break it)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Keeper——Go 的嵌入式秘密存储（帮助我破解它）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47715339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47715339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Keeper offers an embedded secret store for Go, designed for local secrecy and simplicity, with an open invitation for security researchers to audit and attempt to break it. The project aims to surface security insights through challenge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Keeper 为 Go 提供嵌入式秘密存储，主打本地隐私与易用性，并公开邀请安全研究者审计与尝试破解。此举旨在通过挑战暴露安全隐患。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨机器人在汽车行业中的应用与挑战，涵盖制造、回收等场景中机器人对汽车的处理与操作能力提升。凸显日益强大的机器人对各行业的颠覆潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47747017' data-tags='AI,Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adlrocha.substack.com/p/adlrocha-how-the-ai-loser-may-end' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s accidental moat: How the &quot;AI Loser&quot; may end up winning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的意外护城河：AI 落后者也许会赢</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47747017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47747017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s cautious stance on AI could become a durable moat, built on privacy, on-device AI, and a tightly integrated ecosystem. As competitors chase scale, Apple&#x27;s trust advantage and user experience may prove sturdier over the long term.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果对 AI 热潮的谨慎态度可能成为长期护城河，源自隐私保护、设备端 AI 与紧密的生态系统。随着竞争对手追逐规模，苹果在信任与用户体验方面的优势或在长期内更为稳固。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN project demonstrates using Unicode characters to hide data inside plain text, exploring practical techniques and detection challenges. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 项目展示了如何在纯文本中利用 Unicode 字符进行隐写，将数据隐藏在看似普通的文本里，并探讨了检测的难点。该方案揭示了打字排版中的微妙技巧所能承载的载荷，同时带来新颖性与安全性方面的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers relying on Heroku experience changes in service, pricing, or support that complicate app hosting and deployment strategies. The note raises questions about platform dependency, migration options, and the resilience of cloud-backed workflows.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://transformer-circuits.pub/2026/emotions/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emotion Concepts and Their Function in a Large Language Model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型中的情感概念及其功能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47637580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47637580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It argues that even if emotions are not truly felt, modelling them can guide safer and more relatable responses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章探讨大型语言模型是否具备显式的情感概念，以及这些概念在推理、对齐和用户体验中的作用。即使模型不真的有情感，建模情感也能引导更安全、易于理解的回复。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/4RH1T3CT0R7/ttf-doom' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: TTF-DOOM – A raycaster running inside TrueType font hinting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：TTF-DOOM——在 TrueType 字体提示处理中运行的光线投射渲染器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47665685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47665685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TTF-DOOM is a playful experiment that runs a raycaster inside TrueType font hinting, turning font hinting into a tiny compute sandbox for visuals. It showcases creative use of typography pipelines and could inspire niche demonstrations of how fonts can carry computational ideas. For enthusiasts, it’s a proof-of-concept bridging fonts and retro rendering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TTF-DOOM 将光线投射渲染器嵌入到 TrueType 字体提示处理中，利用字体 hinting 进行小规模计算与渲染的实验。展示了排版管线的创造性用法，可能启发将字体与计算概念结合的有趣演示。对爱好者来说，这是字体与光线投射的概念验证。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f7f6be128b5f08bbbd8d785bb0a7c661f02e463834e689c13c84f2dde8d536a5/4RH1T3CT0R7/ttf-doom' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f7f6be128b5f08bbbd8d785bb0a7c661f02e463834e689c13c84f2dde8d536a5/4RH1T3CT0R7/ttf-doom' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47657699' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(413) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Sidenai/sidex' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SideX – A Tauri-based port of Visual Studio Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SideX：一个基于 Tauri 的 Visual Studio Code 端口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47657699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47657699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SideX is a Visual Studio Code-like editor built on Tauri, offering a native-like VS Code experience with a lighter footprint and improved security isolation. By leveraging Rust and a minimal runtime, it avoids Electron bloat while maintaining familiar workflows. This matters for developers who want a self-contained editor without heavy dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SideX 是一个基于 Tauri 的 Visual Studio Code 风格编辑器，提供接近原生的体验，同时降低资源占用并提升安全隔离。通过使用 Rust 与更小的运行时，它摆脱了 Electron 的臃肿并保持熟悉的工作流。对于想要无需大量依赖的自包含编辑器的开发者，这很有吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c7f48f5e2af966608ec53267142278d6f6faa1d782bcbd630d1abc1056e419a8/Sidenai/sidex' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c7f48f5e2af966608ec53267142278d6f6faa1d782bcbd630d1abc1056e419a8/Sidenai/sidex' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47729477' data-tags='Show HN,Gaming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hormuz Havoc, a satirical game, was overrun by AI bots within 24 hours, illustrating how open platforms can be rapidly flooded by automated agents. The incident spotlights bot resilience in online environments and the need for stronger anti-bot defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hormuz Havoc 这款讽刺性游戏在24小时内就被 AI 机器人席卷，显示开放平台容易被自动化代理迅速占据的风险。此次事件凸显在线环境中对抗机器人攻击的难度与对更强反 bot 措施的需求。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47665207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47665207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SOM is a minimal Smalltalk-inspired environment designed for teaching and VM research, serving as an approachable platform to explore object-oriented VM concepts. Its compact design and educational focus make it useful for classroom labs and experimental studies in virtual machines. It can help illustrate language runtimes and OS-level ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SOM 是一个极简的 Smalltalk 风格环境，面向教学与虚拟机研究，便于学习面向对象的虚拟机概念。它的紧凑设计和教育目标适合课堂实验与虚拟机相关的研究，帮助学生理解语言运行时与操作系统层面的思路。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(430) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://netbsd-cells.petermann-digital.de/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cells for NetBSD: kernel-enforced, jail-like isolation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NetBSD 的 Cells：内核级隔离</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47680532' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47680532' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD&#x27;s Cells offer kernel-enforced isolation that behaves like a jail, allowing multiple secure compartments within a single OS instance. This enables safer multi-tenant setups and tighter control over resource usage without heavy virtualization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 的 Cells 提供内核强制的隔离，类似于 jail 的分区，允许在同一操作系统实例中部署多个安全环境。这为多租户场景提供更安全的资源边界，同时降低虚拟化开销。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://netbsd-cells.petermann-digital.de/images/cover-image.jpg' data-full='https://netbsd-cells.petermann-digital.de/images/cover-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(433) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/vefsxkGWkEMmDcZ7v/the-effects-of-caffeine-consumption-do-not-decay-with-a-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The effects of caffeine consumption do not decay with a ~5 hour half-life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>咖啡因效应并不随约5小时半衰期衰减</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47717973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47717973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A LessWrong piece argues caffeine&#x27;s effects persist beyond a simple half-life model, with lingering wakefulness and performance impacts that don&#x27;t neatly decay. This reframes personal caffeine strategies and experimental design around stimulant use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇 LessWrong 文章指出，咖啡因的效应并不符合简单的半衰期模型，清醒与表现的影响可能持续超出预期。此观点将重塑个人咖啡因使用策略与实验设计。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47737542' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(434) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Optimization of 32-bit Unsigned Division by Constants on 64-bit Targets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在64位目标上对32位无符号常数除法的优化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes an optimization strategy for performing 32-bit unsigned division by constants on 64-bit targets, using precomputed multipliers and shifts to replace costly division instructions. The technique can help compilers generate faster code for numeric-heavy workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在64位目标上将32位无符号常数除法替换为乘法因子和位移的优化方法，避免昂贵的除法指令。该技巧有助于编译器生成对数值密集型负载更高效的代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Map Gesture Controls demonstrates translating hand gestures into map interactions, enabling intuitive navigation without clicking. It&#x27;s a practical example of gesture-driven UX for complex interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>地图手势控制展示了如何将手势转化为地图交互，实现无鼠标的直观导航。该项目利用计算机视觉或传感器解析手势，并将其映射到平移、缩放与旋转等操作。为复杂界面提供了手势驱动的可用性实践示例。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
