<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47684811' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://merylldindin.com/thoughts/company-brain/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>One Brain to Query: Wiring a 60-Person Company into a Single Slack Bot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个公司大脑：把60人的公司接入到单一 Slack 机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47684811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47684811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A &#x27;company brain&#x27; concept wires people, documents, and workflows into a single Slack bot for company-wide querying. The approach reduces context-switching and speeds up decision-making by providing instant access to people, policies, and project status.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“公司大脑”将人员、文档和工作流程整合到一个 Slack 机器人中，提升全员查询与协作的效率。该方法可以减少上下文切换、加速决策，便于快速获取人员信息、政策与项目状态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.merylldindin.com/thumbnails/company-brain-20260330T113044.webp' data-full='https://cdn.merylldindin.com/thumbnails/company-brain-20260330T113044.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/simplest-hash-functions/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simplest Hash Functions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最简单的哈希函数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ultra-simple hash functions can perform surprisingly well for non-adversarial tasks. The post surveys a few minimal constructions, analyzes their distribution and collision behavior, and explains where they are appropriate (like simple hash tables) and where they fail under attack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>极其简单的哈希函数在非对抗场景下往往效果不错。文章演示了几种最简构造，分析分布与碰撞特性，并指出在哈希表等场景的适用性及在对抗性输入下的局限。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://michael.stapelberg.ch/posts/2026-04-05-stamp-it-all-programs-must-report-their-version/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stamp It: All programs must report their version</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stamp It：所有程序必须暴露版本信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47655978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47655978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stamp It argues that every program should publish a version stamp at startup to improve reproducibility, debugging, and security auditing. It offers practical strategies for embedding version metadata and discusses trade-offs for small scripts or embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stamp It 提出每个程序在启动时都应暴露版本信息，以提升可重复性、调试和安全审计能力。文中给出嵌入版本元数据的实用策略，并讨论小脚本或嵌入式系统的取舍。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.typotheque.com/blog/zed-a-sans-for-the-needs-of-21century' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zed, A sans for the needs of 21st century</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zed：面向21世纪需求的无衬线字体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zed is Typotheque&#x27;s sans-serif designed for modern reading needs, with features like high legibility on screens, versatile weights, and multilingual support. It showcases how typography aims to balance readability, aesthetic versatility, and web-friendliness in a global context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zed 是 Typotheque 设计的现代无衬线字体，强调在屏幕上提高可读性、提供多样的粗细与广泛的语言支持。它体现了在全球化语境下追求可读性、美学灵活性与网页友好性的字体设计思路。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pilgrima.ge/p/the-grand-line' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why meaningful days look like nothing while you are living them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有意义的日子在当下看起来平淡无奇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47735810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47735810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meaningful days often feel ordinary in real time; significance emerges in hindsight. The piece invites readers to slow down and savor small moments, or capture them to preserve memory, since meaning accrues with reflection rather than immediate awe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有意义的日子在当下常常显得平淡，真正的价值往往在事后回看时才浮现。文章鼓励人们放慢脚步，留意日常中的细小瞬间，或通过记录来保存记忆，因为意义是在反思中积累的。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://beehiiv-images-production.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/asset/file/98ef9a0b-4fbb-4981-a27a-3aa1630b0041/og-image.jpg?t=1775960592' data-full='https://beehiiv-images-production.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/asset/file/98ef9a0b-4fbb-4981-a27a-3aa1630b0041/og-image.jpg?t=1775960592' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A classic 1970s text adventure, Haunt, is now playable on the web, letting modern players experience parser-driven storytelling without any setup. The project preserves vintage interactive fiction using browser technology, highlighting how retro games can reach new audiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经典的1970年代文本冒险游戏 Haunt 现已在网页上可玩，现代玩家无需额外设置即可体验解析器驱动的叙事。这一项目通过浏览器技术实现对老式交互式小说的保存与传播，展示了复古游戏的跨世代可访问性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(467) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chessapig.github.io/talks/Big-Small' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is Math Big or Small?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学到底是大还是小？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A talk that challenges whether mathematical ideas live at a &#x27;big&#x27; scale or can be built from small, composable units. It sketches how mathematical abstractions scale in theory and computation, with implications for teaching, formalization, and tool-building.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论数学到底是由少数“大”概念驱动，还是由大量可组合的小概念构成。演讲主张将数学视作模块化领域，强调简单且易理解的基本单元如何组合出复杂结构，并探讨对教学、形式化和工具开发的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(471) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/1080192.1080202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sybilproof reputation mechanisms (2005) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>防 Sybil 攻击的声誉机制（2005）[PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This classic 2005 paper proposes reputation-based mechanisms to prevent Sybil attacks in distributed systems. It remains influential for understanding how to build trust in decentralized networks and has informed later work in P2P systems and blockchain governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇 2005 年的经典论文提出了基于声誉的机制来防止分布式系统中的 Sybil 攻击。它对在去中心化网络中建立信任的理解影响深远，也为后续的 P2P 与区块链治理研究提供了参考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-06/nyc-families-need-over-125-000-in-income-to-live-in-any-borough' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NYC Families Need over $125,000 in Income to Live in Any Borough</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市家庭在任何区都需年收入超过125,000美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47667459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47667459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bloomberg reports that NYC families need more than $125k in annual income to live in any borough, highlighting housing costs and regional affordability challenges. The figure matters for tech workers and urban policy, shaping where talent can afford to live and how teams recruit locally. It underscores the gap between wages and living expenses in major cities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>彭博报道，纽约市家庭在任何一个区都需要超过12.5万美元的年收入才能居住，反映出住房成本与地区可负担性的挑战。这一数字对科技工作者与城市政策影响重大，决定了人才的居住地点与招聘策略。揭示大城市薪资与生活成本之间的差距。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yaseenghanem.com/recovery-unrestricted-write-access/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Root Persistence via macOS Recovery Mode Safari</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过 macOS 恢复模式的 Safari 实现根权限持久性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47666767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47666767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A macOS Safari in Recovery Mode weakness could grant persistent root write access, enabling attackers to survive reboots and potentially evade standard security controls. The disclosure underscores the attack surface in recovery environments and the need for mitigations and secure defaults. It reminds defenders to harden recovery workflows and monitor for abuse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了在 macOS 恢复模式下的 Safari 漏洞可实现对根权限的持久性，攻击者可在重启后保持访问并规避常见安全控制。这暴露了恢复环境的攻击面，也强调了改进默认设置与缓解策略的必要性。提醒防守方加强恢复工作流的安全性并监控潜在滥用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yaseenghanem.com/content/images/2026/04/image.png' data-full='https://yaseenghanem.com/content/images/2026/04/image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates running ACE on a USB‑HDMI adapter, showcasing hardware‑level compatibility and potential for compact AI or compute tasks on consumer hardware. The experiment hints at how off‑the‑shelf adapters could support lightweight workloads without dedicated GPUs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 USB‑HDMI 适配器上实现 ACE 的演示，展示了商用硬件的兼容性及在小型设备上执行轻量化工作负载的潜力。该实验暗示了现成适配器在不依赖专用 GPU 的情况下也能支持某些计算任务的可能性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(483) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/apr/05/how-paris-swapped-cars-for-bikes-and-remade-its-streets' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Paris swapped cars for bikes – and transformed its streets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴黎如何用自行车占据街头并改造街区</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47658772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47658772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paris&#x27;s shift from cars to bikes, with redesigned streets and safer cycling networks, reshapes urban mobility and reduces emissions. The transformation offers a blueprint for other cities pursuing livable, tech-enabled streets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴黎将街道从汽车转向自行车，改造了道路并提升了骑行安全，重塑了城市出行并降低排放。这一转变为其他城市打造宜居、以科技赋能的街区提供了可借鉴的蓝本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/77e512c0c8c08500b87c0b8a533d3f6a46fc95fd/0_0_7129_5706/master/7129.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0819d32a27c42466004865aa8feaf302' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/77e512c0c8c08500b87c0b8a533d3f6a46fc95fd/0_0_7129_5706/master/7129.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0819d32a27c42466004865aa8feaf302' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(487) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.msn.com/en-my/news/other/meta-is-set-to-pay-its-top-ai-executives-almost-a-billion-each-in-bonuses-if-they-hit-their-targets/ar-AA1ZszqA' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta is set to pay its top AI executives almost a billion each in bonuses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 将向其顶级 AI 高管支付近十亿美元的奖金</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47729975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47729975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta plans nearly $1B in bonuses for its top AI executives if targets are met, underscoring how AI milestones are tying executive compensation to performance. The plan highlights the high-stakes AI race and raises questions about incentive structures and long-term governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 计划在达成目标的情况下向其顶级 AI 高管支付近 10 亿美元奖金，凸显将 AI 里程碑与高管绩效挂钩的激励机制。此举反映出 AI 竞争的高风险性，同时引发对激励结构与长期治理的思考。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cato.org/commentary/hungary-laboratory-illiberal-nationalism-results-are' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hungary Is a Laboratory for Illiberal Nationalism. The Results Are In</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>匈牙利是 illiberal nationalism 的实验室，结果已出炉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47729926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47729926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis argues Hungary serves as a lab for illiberal nationalism, with evidence of democratic backsliding and centralized power. For tech policy readers, the piece highlights how governance models shape digital rights, platform regulation, and data stewardship across society.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析认为匈牙利是非自由民粹民族主义的试验场，存在民主倒退与权力高度集中等证据。对科技与政策读者而言，该文章强调治理模式如何影响数字权利、平台监管与全社会的数据治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cato.org/sites/cato.org/files/styles/optimized/public/2026-03/Viktor%20Orb%C3%A1n%E2%80%99s%20Hungary%20Updated.png?itok=HKEkgxcR' data-full='https://www.cato.org/sites/cato.org/files/styles/optimized/public/2026-03/Viktor%20Orb%C3%A1n%E2%80%99s%20Hungary%20Updated.png?itok=HKEkgxcR' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Docking is an extensible Linux dock implemented in Python, enabling plugin-like extensions to customize panel behavior. It showcases rapid, Python-based customization in the Linux desktop space, appealing to power users who want fast, keyboard-driven data work without GUI overhead. If you value extensibility and Python tooling, you’ll find this interesting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Docking 是一个用 Python 实现的可扩展 Linux Dock，支持通过插件方式自定义面板行为。它展示了在 Linux 桌面领域快速、基于 Python 的定制能力，适合追求高可扩展性的高级用户。若你看重可扩展性与 Python 工具链，这个项目值得关注。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(492) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/ce84rvx0e6do' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Great at gaming? US air traffic control wants you to apply</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国空管局：会玩游戏的你也能来申请</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47727575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47727575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US air traffic control is inviting applicants who excel at gaming, arguing that fast decision-making, pattern recognition, and hand-eye coordination transfer well to radar-based airspace management. The piece outlines the recruitment push, training pathway, and how a gamer mindset can translate into a regulated, high-stakes role.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国空中交通管制部门正在招募擅长游戏的人才，声称快速决策、模式识别和手眼协调可迁移到雷达监控的空域管理工作。文章介绍招募计划、培训路径，以及游戏思维在受监管的高风险岗位中的潜在作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexrios.me/blog/slogbox-devlog/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building Slogbox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>构建 Slogbox</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Devlog chronicles the building of Slogbox, detailing architecture decisions, challenges, and lessons learned for anyone crafting a similar tooling project.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发日志记录了构建 Slogbox 的过程，描述架构决策、遇到的挑战，以及对同类工具项目的经验教训。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47727974' data-tags='AI,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(494) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/teslas-self-driving-software-gets-dutch-go-ahead-boost-eu-ambitions-2026-04-10/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla&#x27;s supervised self-driving software gets Dutch okay, first in Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰批准特斯拉的受监管自动驾驶，欧洲首例</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47727974' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47727974' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dutch regulators give Tesla&#x27;s supervised self-driving software the green light, marking Europe’s first approval for this kind of system. The move signals regulatory progress toward broader, safer deployment of driver-assistance tech and could influence EU policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰监管机构批准特斯拉的受监管自动驾驶软件，成为欧洲第一批获批此类系统的市场。这一进展可能推动更广泛且更安全的驾驶辅助技术在欧盟的部署与监管政策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47729727' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(495) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seas.upenn.edu/stories/penn-researchers-use-ai-to-surface-unreported-glp-1-side-effects-in-reddit-posts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Researchers used AI to analyze 400k Reddit posts, revealing GLP-1 side effects</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究人员使用 AI 分析 40 万条 Reddit 帖子，揭示 GLP-1 副作用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47729727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47729727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI analysis of 400k Reddit posts surfaces unreported GLP-1 drug side effects, offering early signals for pharmacovigilance beyond official reports. The approach shows the potential of social-media mining for real-world safety monitoring, while noting the need for clinical validation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 AI 分析 40 万条 Reddit 帖子，揭示 GLP-1 药物的未被官方报道的副作用，提供早期的药物安全信号。该方法展示了利用社交媒体数据进行真实世界安全监测的潜力，同时需进行临床验证。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.seas.upenn.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/AI-and-Reddit-GLP-1s.jpg' data-full='https://www.seas.upenn.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/AI-and-Reddit-GLP-1s.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
